# Keys Synth — Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task, **inline in one session** (the user asked for no subagents). Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Replace the built-in keys synth with a semi-modular wavetable synthesizer (oscillators with unison and warp, Moog-style ladder and state-variable filters, three envelopes, two LFOs, a 16-slot modulation matrix, an FX chain), played from the MIDI keyboard, shaped from a synth plate on the PLAY screen (sharing its plate with a slim mixer) and from the Launch Control XL knobs, with live visualizers of its signal and filter response.

**Architecture:** All DSP lives in a new `lpd-core` module `synth` behind the existing `Instrument` trait, on the same real-time audio path as the pads. The router (Rust) stays the single owner of the set: synth edits go through `AppState::edit` (undo, autosave) and reach the engine as small lock-free commands. The telemetry thread turns a synth tap into a `synth-telemetry` event. The Svelte UI only edits and draws; parameter metadata comes from a TypeScript module generated by a cargo test, so the UI never drifts from the engine.

**Tech Stack:** Rust (lpd-core, Tauri 2, cpal 0.15, midir 0.10, rtrb 0.3, rustysynth 1, serde), Svelte 5 + TypeScript 5.9, Vite 8, `node --test` for `src/lib/*.test.ts`. No new crates.

**Spec:** `../specs/2026-10-24-keys-synth-design.md` (the "spec" below; section numbers like "spec 3.5" refer to it).

**How to read this plan.** Section 0 holds the rules, the decisions made while planning (K1–K20), the shared name registry and the phase list. Every task restates what it implements, fixes every new name (Rust items, commands with their exact JS argument keys, events and payloads, TS types, CSS classes, user-visible strings), gives the code of every DSP kernel and of the pure helpers, lists the tests with expected values, and ends with a "Do not" list and a "Done when" checklist. **If a task and section 0 disagree, section 0 wins; if this plan and the spec disagree, this plan wins (section 0.3 records each deviation). Report any other disagreement instead of choosing silently.**

No synth implementation or UI/hardware checks at baseline.

### 0.2 Invariants (never break)

1. **Audio thread:** no allocation, no locks, no I/O, no frees in `Engine::handle` / `Engine::render` and in everything they call (`Synth`, voices, FX). Everything is allocated in `Synth::new`. Boxes leave through the trash queue. `crates/lpd-core/tests/no_alloc.rs` gets a case for every new engine path.
2. **Single set owner:** the router's `Set` is the only copy. Synth edits go through `AppState::edit` / `edit_at` / `edit_in` (undo + `touch()`), never by writing `r.set_mut()` from a command without history (the two exceptions are named in Task 11: patch save metadata and `modified` after save/revert).
3. **Undo scope** (spec 8.5): synth edits are undoable (knob drags, LCXL turns and learned CCs coalesce per control with key `synth.<id>` or `learn:synth.<id>` within 1 s; a route add / change / delete and a patch load are one step each). Mixer values stay non-undoable (`restore_keeping_mixer`).
4. **Lock order** in `src-tauri`: `router` → `history` → `kit_manager` → `saver`; `painter` is a leaf. Never hold `router` while calling `emit`: clone, drop the guard, then emit. Patch-library file I/O happens with no lock held.
5. **Tauri v2 argument names:** Rust command parameters snake_case; JS passes only camelCase keys. Structs crossing the bridge use `#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]`.
6. **Hardware-sourced events are never re-triggered from the UI** (plan 1C invariant): keys notes from the Keystation reach the engine in Rust; the UI only mirrors `keys-note`.
7. **The look is done for existing parts.** New parts reuse the existing classes (`.plate` + `data-label`, `.screen`, `.lcd`, `.lcd-dim`, `.silk`, `.key`, `.key.on`, `.key.small`, `.switch`, `.lamp`) and tokens (`--orange`, `--warn`, `--ok`, `--error`, `--lcd`, `--lcd-dim`, `--silk`, `--vec`, `--line`, `--glass`, `--focus`, `--knob-track`, `--tag`, fonts `--f-lcd`, `--f-silk`). The Telemetry column must look exactly as before after Task 17's extraction.
8. **Accessibility:** knobs and faders `role="slider"` with `aria-valuetext` in units; toggle keys `aria-pressed`; displays `aria-hidden="true"`; keyboard operation as today's `Knob` (arrows, Shift fine, Home / End, double-click = default).
9. `prefers-reduced-motion`: synth displays, rings and meters update at 10 Hz instead of every frame; no afterglow on the synth scope.
10. Text uses the true minus `−`, `×`, `…`, `·` and `→` exactly as written in this plan.
11. The browser preview renders and throws nothing (section 0.1).

### 0.3 Decisions made while planning (do not revisit)

| # | Decision |
|---|---|
| K1 | Parameter metadata reaches the UI as a generated TypeScript module `src/lib/synth-meta.ts` (written by the cargo test `synth_meta`), not as a JSON file: no JSON-import configuration is needed in Vite, svelte-check or `node --test`. (The spec was updated to name `synth-meta.ts`.) |
| K2 | `knob_pan`, `knob_pitch` and `knob_lowpass` stay in `crates/lpd-core/src/devices/lcxl.rs`: MIDI learn uses them for `group.*` CCs. Only the LCXL itself stops calling them. (Spec 2.1 was corrected to say so when this plan was written.) |
| K3 | `set_keys` now takes `settings: KeysMix` = `{ gmSound, volumeDb, output }`. The patch, its source and `modified` change only through the `synth_*` commands. |
| K4 | The tempo reaches the synth as `Command::KeysTempo(bpm)`, sent by `Router::send_keys()` and whenever `set.pattern.bpm` changes (sequencer edit, learned `seq.bpm`). |
| K5 | `Command::KeysPitch(f32)` carries the normalized pitch wheel −1…+1. The synth scales it by BEND RANGE; `SoundFontKeys` by ±2 semitones. The `Instrument::pitch_bend` parameter becomes `wheel: f32`. |
| K6 | SETUP › KEYS `LOAD…` stores the `.sf2` path **and** switches the engine to SOUNDFONT; `CLEAR` removes the path and switches to SYNTH; `ENGINE: SYNTH` keeps the path so SOUNDFONT can be chosen again. |
| K7 | `KeysStatus.engine` is `"synth"` or `"soundfont"`; for the synth `name` is `"SYNTH"` (the UI prints the patch name from the set). |
| K8 | The compact page set (plate under 480 px tall) is OSC · FILTER · ENV · LFO · FX · MATRIX (spec 6.3 had one MOD page). The MATRIX list scrolls when its rows do not fit; every other page fits without scrolling. |
| K9 | The voice path is stereo: two filter instances per voice share coefficients, so unison WIDTH survives the filter. SUB and NOISE are centred. |
| K10 | FX buffers are sized from the sample rate passed to `Synth::new` (spec 11 said "sized for 96 kHz"). |
| K11 | Voice stealing copies the stolen voice into one of 4 fade slots, which fades it out linearly over 3 ms while the voice slot restarts clean. |
| K12 | A patch load (`SynthLoad`) fades the synth output out over 5 ms, then applies the values, silences every voice, clears the FX buffers and starts the notes that arrived during the fade (up to 16 are held back). |
| K13 | `src/components/MixerStrip.svelte` is deleted; `MixerSlim.svelte` replaces it on PLAY. |
| K14 | Between Task 9 and Task 18 the keys plate's sound LCD picks only `gmSound` (the SoundFont's GM program); the synth plays the set's patch. Task 18 turns the LCD into the patch picker. |
| K15 | Wavetable mipmaps: 10 levels; level `k` keeps `512 >> k` harmonics in a table of `max(4 × harmonics, 256)` samples per frame (about 3.5 MB for the 8 tables; spec 3.3 estimated 8 MB). |
| K16 | Size results of the solver (the spec 6.1 table was corrected to these when this plan was written): 2560×1440 floating → pads 94, synth 854; docked → pads 77, telemetry 220, synth 760; 1920×1080 → pads 56, synth 599 (a docked drawer floats); 1280×720 → pads 37, telemetry and mixer collapsed, synth 561. |
| K17 | Below the 1280×720 minimum window the synth may be narrower than 480 px (no further fallback). |
| K18 | Backend notices about the keys travel as the new event `keys-notice` `{ text }`; the UI shows them as a toast. |
| K19 | FX MIX knobs are dry/wet crossfades (`out = (1 − mix)·dry + mix·wet`); DRIVE MIX 1 is fully driven. The DRIVE unit's wet signal is scaled by `1/√(pre-gain)`. |
| K20 | The engine feeds the keys scope tap from whatever instrument plays (`(l + r) / 2` before the keys volume). `SynthMonitor` comes from `Instrument::monitor()`, which only the synth implements; `synth-telemetry` is emitted only while a monitor arrived in the last 250 ms. |
| K21 | User decision (2026-10-24): keep `#[cfg_attr(debug_assertions, ignore)]`; run release timing tests by name or without `--ignored`, because release builds do not ignore these tests. Applies to Task 2, Task 8 and Task 19 and overrides the original prompt release commands. |
| K22 | User decision (2026-10-24): FX idle detection must also check pending sound in delay buffers and filter/FIR states, so silent gaps before echoes do not freeze the effect. Delay lines track samples at or above `1e-6` in constant time on write; enabled units sleep only when input/previous output are quiet and no pending state remains. Overrides Task 8 original idle rule and code. |
| K23 | Task 8 numerical test correction under prompt rule 2: the second ping-pong impulse is `0.5 * (1 - exp(-2π*6000/48000)) = 0.272031`, so its assertion uses that value rather than `> 0.3`. The specified 6 kHz tone, 0.5 feedback and DSP remain unchanged. |
| K24 | User decision (2026-10-24): preserve Task 11 strict rejection of any filename containing `..`; file_name replaces double dots with underscores (`A..B` → `A__B.lbpatch`) while preserving the displayed patch name. Task 11 also updates patch.rs and its filename test. |

## Phase A — Synth engine (`lpd-core`)

Phase A builds the synth as a self-contained module. Nothing in the app uses it until Task 10, so the app keeps working unchanged through Tasks 1–8. Each task adds one file (or two) to `crates/lpd-core/src/synth/` and one integration test file in `crates/lpd-core/tests/`.

### Task 1: Parameter model

**Purpose.** Spec 3 (every parameter table), 4.1, 4.2, 4.4 and 8.1 (the JSON shape). One place that knows every parameter's id, range, taper, unit and default, so the engine, the router, the LCXL, MIDI learn and the UI never disagree.

**Files:**
- Create: `crates/lpd-core/src/synth/mod.rs`, `crates/lpd-core/src/synth/params.rs`, `crates/lpd-core/tests/synth_params.rs`
- Modify: `crates/lpd-core/src/lib.rs` (add `pub mod synth;`)

- [x] **Step 1: Write the failing tests** — `crates/lpd-core/tests/synth_params.rs`:

```rust
use lpd_core::synth::params::*;
use std::collections::HashSet;

fn float(id: ParamId) -> (f32, f32, Taper) {
    match id.meta().kind {
        Kind::Float { min, max, taper, .. } => (min, max, taper),
        other => panic!("{id:?} is not a float: {other:?}"),
    }
}

#[test]
fn every_param_id_round_trips_through_its_string() {
    assert_eq!(ParamId::ALL.len(), 109);
    let mut seen = HashSet::new();
    for &id in ParamId::ALL {
        let meta = id.meta();
        assert!(seen.insert(meta.id), "duplicate id {}", meta.id);
        assert_eq!(ParamId::from_id(meta.id), Some(id));
    }
    assert_eq!(ParamId::from_id("filter.nope"), None);
}

#[test]
fn defaults_match_the_spec() {
    let p = SynthParams::default();
    assert!(p.osc_a.on && !p.osc_b.on);
    assert_eq!((p.osc_a.table, p.osc_a.pos, p.osc_a.unison), (TableId::Basic, 0.667, 1));
    assert_eq!((p.osc_a.detune, p.osc_a.blend, p.osc_a.width), (0.25, 0.75, 0.5));
    assert_eq!((p.osc_a.phase_rand, p.osc_a.level, p.osc_a.pan), (1.0, 0.75, 0.0));
    assert!(!p.sub.on && p.sub.oct == -1 && p.sub.level == 0.6);
    assert!(!p.noise.on && p.noise.level == 0.3);
    assert_eq!((p.filter.kind, p.filter.cutoff, p.filter.reso), (FilterType::Lp24, 12_000.0, 0.0));
    assert_eq!((p.amp.level, p.amp.vel_sens), (0.8, 0.7));
    assert_eq!(p.env[0].sustain, 1.0);
    assert_eq!((p.env[1].sustain, p.env[2].sustain), (0.0, 0.0));
    assert_eq!((p.env[0].attack, p.env[0].decay, p.env[0].release), (0.002, 0.5, 0.15));
    assert_eq!((p.lfo[0].shape, p.lfo[0].rate, p.lfo[0].mode), (LfoShape::Sine, 5.5, LfoMode::Trig));
    assert_eq!((p.lfo[1].shape, p.lfo[1].rate, p.lfo[1].mode), (LfoShape::Triangle, 0.5, LfoMode::Free));
    assert_eq!(p.lfo[0].sync_div, SyncDiv::D1_4);
    assert_eq!((p.voice.mode, p.voice.bend_range, p.voice.volume_db), (VoiceMode::Poly, 2, 0.0));
    assert!(!p.fx.drive.on && !p.fx.chorus.on && !p.fx.delay.on && !p.fx.reverb.on);
    assert_eq!(p.fx.delay.sync_div, SyncDiv::D1_8D);
    assert!(p.routes.is_empty());
}

#[test]
fn tapers_map_known_points() {
    let cutoff = ParamId::FilterCutoff.meta().kind;
    assert!((cutoff.from_norm(0.5) - 632.456).abs() < 0.01);
    assert!((cutoff.to_norm(12_000.0) - 0.926_05).abs() < 1e-4);
    assert!((ParamId::Env1Attack.meta().kind.from_norm(0.5) - 1.25).abs() < 1e-6);
    assert!((ParamId::Lfo1Rate.meta().kind.from_norm(0.5) - 0.707_107).abs() < 1e-5);
    assert!((ParamId::FxDelayTime.meta().kind.from_norm(0.5) - 0.044_721_4).abs() < 1e-6);
    assert!((ParamId::FilterEnvAmt.meta().kind.from_norm(0.5)).abs() < 1e-6);
    for &id in ParamId::ALL {
        if let Kind::Float { min, taper, .. } = id.meta().kind {
            if taper == Taper::Cubic {
                assert_eq!(min, 0.0, "{id:?}: cubic tapers start at 0");
            }
            for n in [0.0, 0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1.0] {
                let k = id.meta().kind;
                assert!((k.to_norm(k.from_norm(n)) - n).abs() < 1e-4, "{id:?} at {n}");
            }
        }
    }
    let _ = float(ParamId::FilterCutoff);
}

#[test]
fn set_clamps_and_quantizes() {
    let mut p = SynthParams::default();
    ParamId::OscAOct.set(&mut p, 5.0);
    assert_eq!(ParamId::OscAOct.get(&p), 3.0);
    ParamId::OscAUnison.set(&mut p, 0.0);
    assert_eq!(p.osc_a.unison, 1);
    ParamId::OscAUnison.set(&mut p, 3.6);
    assert_eq!(p.osc_a.unison, 4);
    ParamId::FilterType.set(&mut p, 9.0);
    assert_eq!(p.filter.kind, FilterType::Notch);
    ParamId::OscBOn.set(&mut p, 0.7);
    assert!(p.osc_b.on);
    ParamId::FilterCutoff.set(&mut p, 5.0);
    assert_eq!(p.filter.cutoff, 20.0);
    ParamId::FilterCutoff.set(&mut p, f32::NAN);
    assert_eq!(p.filter.cutoff, 20.0);
    ParamId::FxDelaySyncDiv.set(&mut p, 14.0);
    assert_eq!(p.fx.delay.sync_div, SyncDiv::D1_2, "the delay stops at 1/2");
    ParamId::SubOct.set(&mut p, 0.0);
    assert_eq!(p.sub.oct, -1);
}

#[test]
fn route_table_appends_replaces_and_compacts() {
    let r = |amount: f32| Route { source: Source::Lfo1, dest: Some(Dest::Cutoff), amount, aux: None, bypass: false };
    let mut t = RouteTable::default();
    assert!(t.push(r(0.1)) && t.push(r(0.2)) && t.push(r(0.3)));
    assert_eq!(t.len(), 3);
    assert!(t.set(1, None));
    assert_eq!(t.len(), 2);
    assert_eq!(t.get(1).unwrap().amount, 0.3);
    assert!(!t.set(5, Some(r(0.5))), "no gaps");
    assert!(t.set(0, Some(r(2.0))));
    assert_eq!(t.get(0).unwrap().amount, 1.0, "amounts are clamped to ±1");
    while t.len() < ROUTES {
        assert!(t.push(r(0.0)));
    }
    assert!(!t.push(r(0.0)), "16 routes at most");
}

#[test]
fn params_serialize_in_the_patch_shape() {
    let p = SynthParams::default();
    let v = serde_json::to_value(p).unwrap();
    for key in ["oscA", "oscB", "sub", "noise", "filter", "amp", "env", "lfo", "voice", "routes", "fx"] {
        assert!(v.get(key).is_some(), "missing {key}");
    }
    assert_eq!(v["filter"]["type"], "lp24");
    assert_eq!(v["env"].as_array().unwrap().len(), 3);
    assert_eq!(v["lfo"][0]["syncDiv"], "1/4");
    assert_eq!(v["routes"], serde_json::json!([]));
    assert_eq!(v["fx"]["delay"]["pingPong"], false);
    let back: SynthParams = serde_json::from_value(v).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(back, p);
}

#[test]
fn missing_fields_take_defaults() {
    let p: SynthParams = serde_json::from_str(r#"{"filter":{"cutoff":300}}"#).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(p.filter.cutoff, 300.0);
    assert_eq!(p.filter.reso, 0.0);
    assert_eq!(p.osc_a, OscParams::default());
    let routed: SynthParams = serde_json::from_str(
        r#"{"routes":[{"source":"lfo1","dest":"osc.pitch","amount":0.021,"aux":"wheel"}]}"#,
    )
    .unwrap();
    let route = routed.routes.get(0).unwrap();
    assert_eq!((route.source, route.dest, route.aux, route.bypass), (Source::Lfo1, Some(Dest::OscPitch), Some(Source::Wheel), false));
}

#[test]
fn destinations_and_sources_are_complete() {
    assert_eq!(Dest::ALL.len(), DESTS);
    assert_eq!(Source::ALL.len(), SOURCES);
    assert_eq!(Dest::Cutoff.param(), Some(ParamId::FilterCutoff));
    assert_eq!(Dest::OscBWarp.param(), Some(ParamId::OscBWarpAmt));
    assert_eq!(Dest::Lfo2Rate.param(), Some(ParamId::Lfo2Rate));
    for d in [Dest::OscAPitch, Dest::OscBPitch, Dest::OscPitch] {
        assert!(d.param().is_none() && d.is_pitch());
    }
    assert_eq!(Dest::IDS[0], "oscA.pos");
    assert_eq!(Dest::IDS[26], "lfo2.rate");
    assert_eq!(Source::Lfo2.color(), "#7f8cff");
}
```

`crates/lpd-core/src/synth/params.rs` (complete):

```rust
//! Synth parameters (keys synth spec §3–5, §8.1): the values of a patch, their ids, ranges,
//! tapers and units. Everything here is plain data; the audio thread reads and writes it
//! without allocating.

use serde::{Deserialize, Deserializer, Serialize, Serializer};

pub const ROUTES: usize = 16;
pub const UNISON_MAX: usize = 7;
pub const ENVS: usize = 3;
pub const LFOS: usize = 2;
pub const DESTS: usize = 27;
pub const SOURCES: usize = 10;

/// A field a `ParamId` reads and writes as `f32` (bools 0 / 1, selectors by index).
pub trait ParamField: Copy {
    fn to_f32(self) -> f32;
    fn from_f32(v: f32) -> Self;
}

impl ParamField for f32 {
    fn to_f32(self) -> f32 {
        self
    }
    fn from_f32(v: f32) -> Self {
        v
    }
}

impl ParamField for bool {
    fn to_f32(self) -> f32 {
        if self {
            1.0
        } else {
            0.0
        }
    }
    fn from_f32(v: f32) -> Self {
        v >= 0.5
    }
}

impl ParamField for i8 {
    fn to_f32(self) -> f32 {
        f32::from(self)
    }
    fn from_f32(v: f32) -> Self {
        v.round() as i8
    }
}

impl ParamField for u8 {
    fn to_f32(self) -> f32 {
        f32::from(self)
    }
    fn from_f32(v: f32) -> Self {
        v.round().max(0.0) as u8
    }
}

/// A selector: its serde ids and the labels the UI prints, in index order.
pub trait Choice: Copy + 'static {
    const IDS: &'static [&'static str];
    const LABELS: &'static [&'static str];
}

macro_rules! choice {
    ($name:ident { $first:ident = $fid:literal / $flabel:literal $(, $v:ident = $id:literal / $label:literal)* $(,)? }) => {
        #[derive(Serialize, Deserialize, Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Hash, Default)]
        pub enum $name {
            #[default]
            #[serde(rename = $fid)]
            $first,
            $(#[serde(rename = $id)] $v,)*
        }

        impl $name {
            pub const ALL: &'static [$name] = &[$name::$first $(, $name::$v)*];
        }

        impl Choice for $name {
            const IDS: &'static [&'static str] = &[$fid $(, $id)*];
            const LABELS: &'static [&'static str] = &[$flabel $(, $label)*];
        }

        impl ParamField for $name {
            fn to_f32(self) -> f32 {
                self as usize as f32
            }
            fn from_f32(v: f32) -> Self {
                let i = if v.is_finite() { v.round().max(0.0) as usize } else { 0 };
                $name::ALL[i.min($name::ALL.len() - 1)]
            }
        }
    };
}

choice!(TableId { Basic = "basic" / "BASIC", Pwm = "pwm" / "PWM", Harmonics = "harmonics" / "HARMONICS",
    Vowels = "vowels" / "VOWELS", FmSweep = "fmSweep" / "FM SWEEP", SyncSweep = "syncSweep" / "SYNC SWEEP",
    Organ = "organ" / "ORGAN", Digital = "digital" / "DIGITAL" });
choice!(Warp { Off = "off" / "OFF", Sync = "sync" / "SYNC", Bend = "bend" / "BEND", Pwm = "pwm" / "PWM",
    Fm = "fm" / "FM", Rm = "rm" / "RM" });
choice!(SubShape { Sine = "sine" / "SINE", Triangle = "triangle" / "TRI", Saw = "saw" / "SAW", Square = "square" / "SQUARE" });
choice!(NoiseColor { White = "white" / "WHITE", Pink = "pink" / "PINK" });
choice!(FilterType { Lp24 = "lp24" / "LP 24", Lp12 = "lp12" / "LP 12", Hp12 = "hp12" / "HP 12",
    Bp12 = "bp12" / "BP 12", Notch = "notch" / "NOTCH" });
choice!(LfoShape { Sine = "sine" / "SINE", Triangle = "triangle" / "TRI", SawUp = "sawUp" / "SAW UP",
    SawDown = "sawDown" / "SAW DN", Square = "square" / "SQUARE", SampleHold = "sampleHold" / "S&H",
    SmoothRandom = "smoothRandom" / "SMOOTH" });
choice!(SyncDiv { D1_32 = "1/32" / "1/32", D1_16T = "1/16T" / "1/16T", D1_16 = "1/16" / "1/16",
    D1_16D = "1/16D" / "1/16D", D1_8T = "1/8T" / "1/8T", D1_8 = "1/8" / "1/8", D1_8D = "1/8D" / "1/8D",
    D1_4T = "1/4T" / "1/4T", D1_4 = "1/4" / "1/4", D1_4D = "1/4D" / "1/4D", D1_2 = "1/2" / "1/2",
    D1_1 = "1/1" / "1/1", D2_1 = "2/1" / "2/1", D4_1 = "4/1" / "4/1", D8_1 = "8/1" / "8/1" });
choice!(LfoMode { Trig = "trig" / "TRIG", Free = "free" / "FREE", OneShot = "oneShot" / "ONE-SHOT" });
choice!(Polarity { Bi = "bi" / "BI", Uni = "uni" / "UNI" });
choice!(VoiceMode { Poly = "poly" / "POLY", Mono = "mono" / "MONO", Legato = "legato" / "LEGATO" });
choice!(GlideMode { Always = "always" / "ALWAYS", Legato = "legato" / "LEGATO" });
choice!(DriveType { Soft = "soft" / "SOFT", Tube = "tube" / "TUBE", Hard = "hard" / "HARD", Fold = "fold" / "FOLD" });
choice!(Source { Env1 = "env1" / "ENV1", Env2 = "env2" / "ENV2", Env3 = "env3" / "ENV3", Lfo1 = "lfo1" / "LFO1",
    Lfo2 = "lfo2" / "LFO2", Vel = "vel" / "VEL", Note = "note" / "NOTE", Rand = "rand" / "RAND",
    Wheel = "wheel" / "WHEEL", Bend = "bend" / "BEND" });
choice!(Dest { OscAPos = "oscA.pos" / "OSC A · POS", OscAPitch = "oscA.pitch" / "OSC A · PITCH",
    OscAFine = "oscA.fine" / "OSC A · FINE", OscAWarp = "oscA.warpAmt" / "OSC A · WARP",
    OscADetune = "oscA.detune" / "OSC A · DETUNE", OscABlend = "oscA.blend" / "OSC A · BLEND",
    OscAWidth = "oscA.width" / "OSC A · WIDTH", OscALevel = "oscA.level" / "OSC A · LEVEL",
    OscAPan = "oscA.pan" / "OSC A · PAN",
    OscBPos = "oscB.pos" / "OSC B · POS", OscBPitch = "oscB.pitch" / "OSC B · PITCH",
    OscBFine = "oscB.fine" / "OSC B · FINE", OscBWarp = "oscB.warpAmt" / "OSC B · WARP",
    OscBDetune = "oscB.detune" / "OSC B · DETUNE", OscBBlend = "oscB.blend" / "OSC B · BLEND",
    OscBWidth = "oscB.width" / "OSC B · WIDTH", OscBLevel = "oscB.level" / "OSC B · LEVEL",
    OscBPan = "oscB.pan" / "OSC B · PAN",
    OscPitch = "osc.pitch" / "OSC · PITCH", SubLevel = "sub.level" / "SUB · LEVEL",
    NoiseLevel = "noise.level" / "NOISE · LEVEL", Cutoff = "filter.cutoff" / "FILTER · CUTOFF",
    Reso = "filter.reso" / "FILTER · RESO", Drive = "filter.drive" / "FILTER · DRIVE",
    AmpLevel = "amp.level" / "AMP · LEVEL", Lfo1Rate = "lfo1.rate" / "LFO1 · RATE",
    Lfo2Rate = "lfo2.rate" / "LFO2 · RATE" });

/// Badge colours of the sources, in `Source` order (spec 4.1).
pub const SOURCE_COLORS: [&str; SOURCES] = [
    "#ff5b1f", "#ffb21a", "#f2e15b", "#41c8ff", "#7f8cff", "#5cff9d", "#3fe0c5", "#d77bff", "#ff7fb0", "#c9d1d9",
];

/// The delay's divisions: the LFO list up to 1/2 (spec 5).
pub const DELAY_DIV_IDS: &[&str] = &["1/32", "1/16T", "1/16", "1/16D", "1/8T", "1/8", "1/8D", "1/4T", "1/4", "1/4D", "1/2"];

impl Source {
    pub fn color(self) -> &'static str {
        SOURCE_COLORS[self as usize]
    }
}

impl SyncDiv {
    /// Length in beats (a quarter note is one beat).
    pub fn beats(self) -> f32 {
        match self {
            SyncDiv::D1_32 => 0.125,
            SyncDiv::D1_16T => 1.0 / 6.0,
            SyncDiv::D1_16 => 0.25,
            SyncDiv::D1_16D => 0.375,
            SyncDiv::D1_8T => 1.0 / 3.0,
            SyncDiv::D1_8 => 0.5,
            SyncDiv::D1_8D => 0.75,
            SyncDiv::D1_4T => 2.0 / 3.0,
            SyncDiv::D1_4 => 1.0,
            SyncDiv::D1_4D => 1.5,
            SyncDiv::D1_2 => 2.0,
            SyncDiv::D1_1 => 4.0,
            SyncDiv::D2_1 => 8.0,
            SyncDiv::D4_1 => 16.0,
            SyncDiv::D8_1 => 32.0,
        }
    }

    pub fn seconds(self, bpm: f32) -> f32 {
        self.beats() * 60.0 / bpm.max(1.0)
    }
}

impl Dest {
    /// The knob a destination moves; `None` for the three pitch destinations.
    pub fn param(self) -> Option<ParamId> {
        Some(match self {
            Dest::OscAPos => ParamId::OscAPos,
            Dest::OscAFine => ParamId::OscAFine,
            Dest::OscAWarp => ParamId::OscAWarpAmt,
            Dest::OscADetune => ParamId::OscADetune,
            Dest::OscABlend => ParamId::OscABlend,
            Dest::OscAWidth => ParamId::OscAWidth,
            Dest::OscALevel => ParamId::OscALevel,
            Dest::OscAPan => ParamId::OscAPan,
            Dest::OscBPos => ParamId::OscBPos,
            Dest::OscBFine => ParamId::OscBFine,
            Dest::OscBWarp => ParamId::OscBWarpAmt,
            Dest::OscBDetune => ParamId::OscBDetune,
            Dest::OscBBlend => ParamId::OscBBlend,
            Dest::OscBWidth => ParamId::OscBWidth,
            Dest::OscBLevel => ParamId::OscBLevel,
            Dest::OscBPan => ParamId::OscBPan,
            Dest::SubLevel => ParamId::SubLevel,
            Dest::NoiseLevel => ParamId::NoiseLevel,
            Dest::Cutoff => ParamId::FilterCutoff,
            Dest::Reso => ParamId::FilterReso,
            Dest::Drive => ParamId::FilterDrive,
            Dest::AmpLevel => ParamId::AmpLevel,
            Dest::Lfo1Rate => ParamId::Lfo1Rate,
            Dest::Lfo2Rate => ParamId::Lfo2Rate,
            Dest::OscAPitch | Dest::OscBPitch | Dest::OscPitch => return None,
        })
    }

    pub fn is_pitch(self) -> bool {
        matches!(self, Dest::OscAPitch | Dest::OscBPitch | Dest::OscPitch)
    }
}

/// One modulation route (spec 4.4). `dest: None` is a fresh row that does nothing yet.
#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize, Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub struct Route {
    pub source: Source,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub dest: Option<Dest>,
    pub amount: f32,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub aux: Option<Source>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub bypass: bool,
}

/// Up to 16 routes, always a dense prefix (serialized as a list of the used slots).
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Default)]
pub struct RouteTable {
    slots: [Option<Route>; ROUTES],
}

impl RouteTable {
    pub fn len(&self) -> usize {
        self.slots.iter().take_while(|s| s.is_some()).count()
    }

    pub fn is_empty(&self) -> bool {
        self.slots[0].is_none()
    }

    pub fn get(&self, slot: usize) -> Option<&Route> {
        self.slots.get(slot).and_then(|s| s.as_ref())
    }

    pub fn iter(&self) -> impl Iterator<Item = &Route> {
        self.slots.iter().map_while(|s| s.as_ref())
    }

    /// `Some` at an existing slot replaces it, at `len` appends (refused when full or past the
    /// end); `None` removes the slot and moves the later routes up. Amounts are clamped to ±1.
    pub fn set(&mut self, slot: usize, route: Option<Route>) -> bool {
        let len = self.len();
        match route {
            Some(mut r) if slot <= len && slot < ROUTES => {
                r.amount = if r.amount.is_finite() { r.amount.clamp(-1.0, 1.0) } else { 0.0 };
                self.slots[slot] = Some(r);
                true
            }
            Some(_) => false,
            None if slot < len => {
                for i in slot..ROUTES - 1 {
                    self.slots[i] = self.slots[i + 1];
                }
                self.slots[ROUTES - 1] = None;
                true
            }
            None => false,
        }
    }

    pub fn push(&mut self, route: Route) -> bool {
        self.set(self.len(), Some(route))
    }
}

impl Serialize for RouteTable {
    fn serialize<S: Serializer>(&self, s: S) -> Result<S::Ok, S::Error> {
        s.collect_seq(self.iter())
    }
}

impl<'de> Deserialize<'de> for RouteTable {
    fn deserialize<D: Deserializer<'de>>(d: D) -> Result<Self, D::Error> {
        let mut table = RouteTable::default();
        for route in Vec::<Route>::deserialize(d)?.into_iter().take(ROUTES) {
            table.push(route);
        }
        Ok(table)
    }
}

/// Every value of a patch (spec 8.1 `params`). Copy, no strings: the audio thread copies it.
#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize, Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase", default)]
pub struct SynthParams {
    pub osc_a: OscParams,
    pub osc_b: OscParams,
    pub sub: SubParams,
    pub noise: NoiseParams,
    pub filter: FilterParams,
    pub amp: AmpParams,
    pub env: [EnvParams; ENVS],
    pub lfo: [LfoParams; LFOS],
    pub voice: VoiceParams,
    pub routes: RouteTable,
    pub fx: FxParams,
}

impl Default for SynthParams {
    /// INIT: a plain saw into an LP 24 at 12 kHz, no FX, no routes.
    fn default() -> Self {
        let free_env = EnvParams { sustain: 0.0, ..EnvParams::default() };
        SynthParams {
            osc_a: OscParams::default(),
            osc_b: OscParams { on: false, ..OscParams::default() },
            sub: SubParams::default(),
            noise: NoiseParams::default(),
            filter: FilterParams::default(),
            amp: AmpParams::default(),
            env: [EnvParams::default(), free_env, free_env],
            lfo: [
                LfoParams::default(),
                LfoParams { shape: LfoShape::Triangle, rate: 0.5, mode: LfoMode::Free, ..LfoParams::default() },
            ],
            voice: VoiceParams::default(),
            routes: RouteTable::default(),
            fx: FxParams::default(),
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum Taper {
    Lin,
    Log,
    Cubic,
}

/// How the UI prints a value (Task 14 formats each one).
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum Unit {
    Pct,
    Hz,
    Sec,
    Cents,
    Semis,
    Db,
    Pan,
    Deg,
    EnvOct,
    Drive24,
    Drive36,
    Oct,
    Voices,
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub enum Kind {
    Bool,
    Choice { ids: &'static [&'static str], labels: &'static [&'static str] },
    Int { min: i32, max: i32, unit: Unit },
    Float { min: f32, max: f32, taper: Taper, unit: Unit },
}

impl Kind {
    /// The nearest legal value (NaN becomes the minimum).
    pub fn clamp(self, v: f32) -> f32 {
        let v = if v.is_nan() { f32::NEG_INFINITY } else { v };
        match self {
            Kind::Bool => {
                if v >= 0.5 {
                    1.0
                } else {
                    0.0
                }
            }
            Kind::Choice { ids, .. } => v.round().clamp(0.0, (ids.len() - 1) as f32),
            Kind::Int { min, max, .. } => v.round().clamp(min as f32, max as f32),
            Kind::Float { min, max, .. } => v.clamp(min, max),
        }
    }

    /// Value → knob position 0–1.
    pub fn to_norm(self, v: f32) -> f32 {
        let n = match self {
            Kind::Bool => {
                if v >= 0.5 {
                    1.0
                } else {
                    0.0
                }
            }
            Kind::Choice { ids, .. } => {
                if ids.len() > 1 {
                    v / (ids.len() - 1) as f32
                } else {
                    0.0
                }
            }
            Kind::Int { min, max, .. } => (v - min as f32) / (max - min).max(1) as f32,
            Kind::Float { min, max, taper, .. } => match taper {
                Taper::Lin => (v - min) / (max - min),
                Taper::Log => (v.max(min) / min).ln() / (max / min).ln(),
                Taper::Cubic => (v.max(0.0) / max).cbrt(),
            },
        };
        if n.is_nan() {
            0.0
        } else {
            n.clamp(0.0, 1.0)
        }
    }

    /// Knob position 0–1 → value (quantized for bools, selectors and integers).
    pub fn from_norm(self, n: f32) -> f32 {
        let n = if n.is_nan() { 0.0 } else { n.clamp(0.0, 1.0) };
        match self {
            Kind::Bool => {
                if n >= 0.5 {
                    1.0
                } else {
                    0.0
                }
            }
            Kind::Choice { ids, .. } => (n * (ids.len() - 1) as f32).round(),
            Kind::Int { min, max, .. } => (min as f32 + n * (max - min) as f32).round(),
            Kind::Float { min, max, taper, .. } => match taper {
                Taper::Lin => min + n * (max - min),
                Taper::Log => min * (max / min).powf(n),
                Taper::Cubic => max * n * n * n,
            },
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub struct ParamMeta {
    pub id: &'static str,
    /// Section title on the synth plate (also the MIDI learn label prefix).
    pub section: &'static str,
    pub label: &'static str,
    pub kind: Kind,
}

const fn f(min: f32, max: f32, taper: Taper, unit: Unit) -> Kind {
    Kind::Float { min, max, taper, unit }
}
const fn int(min: i32, max: i32, unit: Unit) -> Kind {
    Kind::Int { min, max, unit }
}
const fn pick<T: Choice>() -> Kind {
    Kind::Choice { ids: T::IDS, labels: T::LABELS }
}
const B: Kind = Kind::Bool;
const PCT: Kind = f(0.0, 1.0, Taper::Lin, Unit::Pct);
use Taper::{Cubic, Lin, Log};

macro_rules! params {
    ($($v:ident = $id:literal, $section:literal, $label:literal, [$($path:tt)+], $kind:expr;)+) => {
        /// Every synth control (spec 3 and 5), with its string id (`oscA.pos`, `fx.reverb.mix`, …).
        #[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Hash)]
        pub enum ParamId { $($v),+ }

        impl ParamId {
            pub const ALL: &'static [ParamId] = &[$(ParamId::$v),+];

            pub fn meta(self) -> ParamMeta {
                match self {
                    $(ParamId::$v => ParamMeta { id: $id, section: $section, label: $label, kind: $kind },)+
                }
            }

            pub fn from_id(id: &str) -> Option<ParamId> {
                match id {
                    $($id => Some(ParamId::$v),)+
                    _ => None,
                }
            }

            pub fn get(self, p: &SynthParams) -> f32 {
                match self {
                    $(ParamId::$v => ParamField::to_f32(p.$($path)+),)+
                }
            }

            /// Writes the value clamped and quantized to the control's kind.
            pub fn set(self, p: &mut SynthParams, value: f32) {
                let v = self.meta().kind.clamp(value);
                match self {
                    $(ParamId::$v => p.$($path)+ = ParamField::from_f32(v),)+
                }
            }
        }
    };
}
```

Notes: the macro emits `p.osc_a.on`, `p.env[0].attack`, `p.fx.drive.kind` from the bracketed token trees. `ParamId` variants that share a name with a type (`SubShape`, `NoiseColor`, `FilterType`, `VoiceMode`) are fine: inside `pick::<…>()` the name resolves to the type. If rustfmt reflows the `choice!` invocations, keep its output.

### Task 2: Band-limited wavetables

**Purpose.** Spec 3.3 and K15: the 8 built-in tables, one band-limited copy per octave so playback never aliases, built once at startup.

**Files:**
- Create: `crates/lpd-core/src/synth/wavetable.rs`, `crates/lpd-core/tests/synth_wavetable.rs`
- Modify: `crates/lpd-core/src/audio/fft.rs` (add `fft_complex`), `crates/lpd-core/src/synth/mod.rs` (`pub mod wavetable;`)

- [x] **Step 1: Write the failing tests** — `crates/lpd-core/tests/synth_wavetable.rs`:

```rust
use lpd_core::audio::fft::fft_complex;
use lpd_core::synth::params::TableId;
use lpd_core::synth::wavetable::{build_all, table, Wavetable, LEVELS};

fn spectrum(signal: &[f32]) -> Vec<f32> {
    let mut re = signal.to_vec();
    let mut im = vec![0.0; signal.len()];
    fft_complex(&mut re, &mut im, false);
    re.iter().zip(&im).take(signal.len() / 2).map(|(r, i)| r.hypot(*i)).collect()
}

#[test]
fn complex_fft_round_trips() {
    let mut re: Vec<f32> = (0..64).map(|i| (i as f32 * 0.37).sin()).collect();
    let original = re.clone();
    let mut im = vec![0.0; 64];
    fft_complex(&mut re, &mut im, false);
    fft_complex(&mut re, &mut im, true);
    for (a, b) in re.iter().zip(&original) {
        assert!((a - b).abs() < 1e-5);
    }
}

#[test]
fn every_level_is_band_limited() {
    for &id in TableId::ALL {
        let t = table(id);
        for frame in [0, t.frames() - 1] {
            for level in 0..LEVELS {
                let mags = spectrum(t.level_data(level, frame));
                let peak = mags.iter().cloned().fold(0.0, f32::max);
                let above = mags.iter().skip(Wavetable::harmonics(level) + 1).cloned().fold(0.0, f32::max);
                assert!(above <= 1e-4 * peak, "{id:?} frame {frame} level {level}: {above} vs {peak}");
            }
        }
    }
}

#[test]
fn frames_are_normalized() {
    for &id in TableId::ALL {
        let t = table(id);
        for frame in 0..t.frames() {
            let peak = t.level_data(0, frame).iter().fold(0.0f32, |m, v| m.max(v.abs()));
            assert!((0.99..=1.01).contains(&peak), "{id:?} frame {frame}: {peak}");
        }
    }
}

#[test]
fn basic_starts_with_a_pure_sine() {
    let mags = spectrum(table(TableId::Basic).level_data(0, 0));
    let fundamental = mags[1];
    assert!(mags.iter().enumerate().filter(|&(k, _)| k != 1).all(|(_, &m)| m < 1e-4 * fundamental));
}

#[test]
fn high_notes_do_not_alias() {
    // A saw at exactly bin 171 of a 2048-point FFT at 48 kHz (about 4 kHz).
    let rate = 48_000.0;
    let hz = rate * 171.0 / 2048.0;
    let t = table(TableId::Basic);
    let level = Wavetable::level_for(hz, rate);
    let mut phase = 0.0f32;
    let mut x = vec![0.0f32; 2048];
    for (i, v) in x.iter_mut().enumerate() {
        let w = 0.5 - 0.5 * (std::f32::consts::TAU * i as f32 / 2048.0).cos();
        *v = t.read(level, 0.667, phase) * w;
        phase = (phase + hz / rate).fract();
    }
    let mags = spectrum(&x);
    let total: f32 = mags.iter().map(|m| m * m).sum();
    let stray: f32 = mags
        .iter()
        .enumerate()
        .filter(|&(k, _)| (1..12).all(|h| (k as i64 - (171 * h) as i64).abs() > 3))
        .map(|(_, m)| m * m)
        .sum();
    // A naive saw would leave aliases around −20 dB; the band-limited one stays under −50 dB.
    assert!(stray < 1e-5 * total, "aliasing energy {}", stray / total);
}

#[test]
fn level_for_picks_the_highest_safe_level() {
    assert_eq!(Wavetable::level_for(20.0, 48_000.0), 0);
    assert_eq!(Wavetable::level_for(440.0, 48_000.0), 4);
    assert_eq!(Wavetable::level_for(4_000.0, 48_000.0), 7);
    assert_eq!(Wavetable::level_for(30_000.0, 48_000.0), LEVELS - 1);
}

#[test]
fn display_frames_have_the_requested_size() {
    let frames = table(TableId::Pwm).display(128);
    assert_eq!(frames.len(), 32);
    assert!(frames.iter().all(|f| f.len() == 128));
}

#[test]
#[cfg_attr(debug_assertions, ignore)]
fn the_tables_build_within_200_ms() {
    let t0 = std::time::Instant::now();
    let tables = build_all();
    assert_eq!(tables.len(), 8);
    assert!(t0.elapsed().as_millis() < 200, "{:?}", t0.elapsed());
}
```

- [x] **Step 3: Implement.** Append to `crates/lpd-core/src/audio/fft.rs`:

```rust
/// In-place radix-2 FFT of `re + i·im` (same power-of-two length). `inverse` uses e^{+i} and
/// divides by n. Allocation-free; for building tables at startup, not for the audio thread.
pub fn fft_complex(re: &mut [f32], im: &mut [f32], inverse: bool) {
    let n = re.len();
    assert!(n.is_power_of_two() && im.len() == n, "fft_complex needs equal power-of-two lengths");
    if n < 2 {
        return;
    }
    let bits = n.trailing_zeros();
    for i in 0..n {
        let j = i.reverse_bits() >> (usize::BITS - bits);
        if j > i {
            re.swap(i, j);
            im.swap(i, j);
        }
    }
    let sign = if inverse { 1.0 } else { -1.0 };
    let mut len = 2;
    while len <= n {
        let (ws, wc) = (sign * std::f64::consts::TAU / len as f64).sin_cos();
        for start in (0..n).step_by(len) {
            let (mut wr, mut wi) = (1.0f64, 0.0f64);
            for k in 0..len / 2 {
                let (a, b) = (start + k, start + k + len / 2);
                let tr = f64::from(re[b]) * wr - f64::from(im[b]) * wi;
                let ti = f64::from(re[b]) * wi + f64::from(im[b]) * wr;
                re[b] = (f64::from(re[a]) - tr) as f32;
                im[b] = (f64::from(im[a]) - ti) as f32;
                re[a] = (f64::from(re[a]) + tr) as f32;
                im[a] = (f64::from(im[a]) + ti) as f32;
                let next = wr * wc - wi * ws;
                wi = wr * ws + wi * wc;
                wr = next;
            }
        }
        len *= 2;
    }
    if inverse {
        let s = 1.0 / n as f32;
        re.iter_mut().chain(im.iter_mut()).for_each(|v| *v *= s);
    }
}
```

`crates/lpd-core/src/synth/wavetable.rs` (complete):

```rust
//! Band-limited wavetables (keys synth spec §3.3, plan K15). Built once at startup, read-only
//! afterwards; the audio thread only reads them.

use super::params::TableId;
use crate::audio::fft::fft_complex;
use std::f32::consts::TAU;
use std::sync::OnceLock;

/// Samples of a source cycle (the time-domain generators are sampled at this resolution).
pub const FRAME: usize = 2048;
/// Band-limited copies per frame, one per octave.
pub const LEVELS: usize = 10;
/// Harmonics kept by level 0.
pub const TOP_HARMONIC: usize = 512;
const MIN_LEN: usize = 256;

pub struct Wavetable {
    frames: usize,
    /// Per level: samples per frame, and the frames back to back, each followed by a copy of its
    /// first sample (so interpolation never wraps).
    levels: Vec<(usize, Vec<f32>)>,
}

impl Wavetable {
    /// Harmonics kept by `level`.
    pub fn harmonics(level: usize) -> usize {
        (TOP_HARMONIC >> level).max(1)
    }

    /// Samples per frame at `level`.
    pub fn level_len(level: usize) -> usize {
        (4 * Self::harmonics(level)).max(MIN_LEN)
    }

    pub fn frames(&self) -> usize {
        self.frames
    }

    /// The first level whose top harmonic stays below Nyquist for a fundamental of `hz`.
    pub fn level_for(hz: f32, rate: f32) -> usize {
        let limit = rate / (2.0 * hz.max(1e-3));
        (0..LEVELS).find(|&k| Self::harmonics(k) as f32 <= limit).unwrap_or(LEVELS - 1)
    }

    /// `pos` 0–1 across the frames (neighbours crossfaded), `phase` 0–1 inside a frame (linear).
    #[inline]
    pub fn read(&self, level: usize, pos: f32, phase: f32) -> f32 {
        let (len, data) = &self.levels[level];
        let (len, stride) = (*len, *len + 1);
        let fp = pos.clamp(0.0, 1.0) * (self.frames - 1) as f32;
        let f0 = (fp as usize).min(self.frames - 1);
        let f1 = (f0 + 1).min(self.frames - 1);
        let ff = fp - f0 as f32;
        let x = phase.clamp(0.0, 1.0) * len as f32;
        let i = (x as usize).min(len - 1);
        let fr = x - i as f32;
        let a = f0 * stride + i;
        let b = f1 * stride + i;
        let va = data[a] + (data[a + 1] - data[a]) * fr;
        let vb = data[b] + (data[b + 1] - data[b]) * fr;
        va + (vb - va) * ff
    }

    /// One frame of one level (without the wrap sample).
    pub fn level_data(&self, level: usize, frame: usize) -> &[f32] {
        let (len, data) = &self.levels[level];
        let start = frame * (len + 1);
        &data[start..start + len]
    }

    /// Every frame of level 0, resampled to `points` samples (for the OSC display).
    pub fn display(&self, points: usize) -> Vec<Vec<f32>> {
        (0..self.frames)
            .map(|f| {
                let pos = if self.frames > 1 { f as f32 / (self.frames - 1) as f32 } else { 0.0 };
                (0..points).map(|i| self.read(0, pos, i as f32 / points as f32)).collect()
            })
            .collect()
    }

    /// `spectrum(frame, h)` = (cosine, sine) amplitude of harmonic `h` (1-based). Every frame is
    /// scaled so that its level-0 copy peaks at 1.0; the same gain applies to all its levels.
    fn from_spectra(frames: usize, spectrum: impl Fn(usize, usize) -> (f32, f32)) -> Wavetable {
        let mut levels: Vec<(usize, Vec<f32>)> = (0..LEVELS)
            .map(|k| {
                let len = Self::level_len(k);
                (len, vec![0.0; frames * (len + 1)])
            })
            .collect();
        let mut re_buf = vec![0.0f32; FRAME];
        let mut im_buf = vec![0.0f32; FRAME];
        for f in 0..frames {
            let spec: Vec<(f32, f32)> = (1..=TOP_HARMONIC).map(|h| spectrum(f, h)).collect();
            let mut gain = 1.0;
            for (k, (len, data)) in levels.iter_mut().enumerate() {
                let len = *len;
                let (re, im) = (&mut re_buf[..len], &mut im_buf[..len]);
                re.fill(0.0);
                im.fill(0.0);
                let half = len as f32 / 2.0;
                for h in 1..=Self::harmonics(k) {
                    let (c, s) = spec[h - 1];
                    re[h] = c * half;
                    im[h] = -s * half;
                    re[len - h] = c * half;
                    im[len - h] = s * half;
                }
                fft_complex(re, im, true);
                if k == 0 {
                    let peak = re.iter().fold(0.0f32, |m, v| m.max(v.abs()));
                    gain = if peak > 1e-9 { 1.0 / peak } else { 1.0 };
                }
                let base = f * (len + 1);
                for n in 0..len {
                    data[base + n] = re[n] * gain;
                }
                data[base + len] = data[base];
            }
        }
        Wavetable { frames, levels }
    }

    /// `cycle(frame, phase)` sampled at FRAME points and analysed into harmonics (DC dropped).
    fn from_cycles(frames: usize, cycle: impl Fn(usize, f32) -> f32) -> Wavetable {
        let mut spectra = vec![(0.0f32, 0.0f32); frames * TOP_HARMONIC];
        let mut re = vec![0.0f32; FRAME];
        let mut im = vec![0.0f32; FRAME];
        let scale = 2.0 / FRAME as f32;
        for f in 0..frames {
            for (n, v) in re.iter_mut().enumerate() {
                *v = cycle(f, n as f32 / FRAME as f32);
            }
            im.fill(0.0);
            fft_complex(&mut re, &mut im, false);
            for h in 1..=TOP_HARMONIC {
                spectra[f * TOP_HARMONIC + h - 1] = (re[h] * scale, -im[h] * scale);
            }
        }
        Self::from_spectra(frames, |f, h| spectra[f * TOP_HARMONIC + h - 1])
    }
}

/// Formants (Hz, bandwidth Hz, gain) of A, E, I, O, U, applied to a 110 Hz saw spectrum.
const VOWELS: [[(f32, f32, f32); 3]; 5] = [
    [(800.0, 80.0, 1.0), (1150.0, 90.0, 0.5), (2900.0, 120.0, 0.25)],
    [(350.0, 80.0, 1.0), (2000.0, 90.0, 0.5), (2800.0, 120.0, 0.25)],
    [(270.0, 80.0, 1.0), (2140.0, 90.0, 0.5), (2950.0, 120.0, 0.25)],
    [(450.0, 80.0, 1.0), (800.0, 90.0, 0.5), (2830.0, 120.0, 0.25)],
    [(325.0, 80.0, 1.0), (700.0, 90.0, 0.5), (2530.0, 120.0, 0.25)],
];

fn vowel_amp(frame: usize, h: usize) -> f32 {
    let hz = 110.0 * h as f32;
    VOWELS[frame].iter().map(|&(fc, bw, g)| g / (1.0 + ((hz - fc) / bw).powi(2))).sum::<f32>() / h as f32
}

/// Drawbars as (harmonic, level): 8′, 4′, 2⅔′, 2′, 1⅗′, 1⅓′, 1′.
const DRAWBARS: [(usize, f32); 7] = [(1, 1.0), (2, 0.6), (3, 0.5), (4, 0.4), (5, 0.3), (6, 0.3), (8, 0.3)];

fn organ_amp(frame: usize, h: usize) -> f32 {
    if frame == 7 {
        return if DRAWBARS.iter().any(|&(dh, _)| dh == h) { 1.0 } else { 0.0 };
    }
    DRAWBARS.iter().take(frame + 1).find(|&&(dh, _)| dh == h).map_or(0.0, |&(_, a)| a)
}

fn build(id: TableId) -> Wavetable {
    match id {
        TableId::Basic => Wavetable::from_cycles(4, |f, p| match f {
            0 => (TAU * p).sin(),
            1 => {
                if p < 0.25 {
                    4.0 * p
                } else if p < 0.75 {
                    2.0 - 4.0 * p
                } else {
                    4.0 * p - 4.0
                }
            }
            2 => 2.0 * p - 1.0,
            _ => {
                if p < 0.5 {
                    1.0
                } else {
                    -1.0
                }
            }
        }),
        TableId::Pwm => Wavetable::from_cycles(32, |f, p| {
            let width = 0.5 - 0.48 * f as f32 / 31.0;
            if p < width {
                1.0
            } else {
                -1.0
            }
        }),
        TableId::Harmonics => {
            Wavetable::from_spectra(32, |f, h| if h <= f + 1 { (0.0, 1.0 / h as f32) } else { (0.0, 0.0) })
        }
        TableId::Vowels => Wavetable::from_spectra(5, |f, h| (0.0, vowel_amp(f, h))),
        TableId::FmSweep => Wavetable::from_cycles(32, |f, p| {
            let index = 10.0 * f as f32 / 31.0;
            (TAU * p + index * (TAU * 2.0 * p).sin()).sin()
        }),
        TableId::SyncSweep => Wavetable::from_cycles(32, |f, p| {
            let ratio = 1.0 + 7.0 * f as f32 / 31.0;
            2.0 * (p * ratio).fract() - 1.0
        }),
        TableId::Organ => Wavetable::from_spectra(8, |f, h| (0.0, organ_amp(f, h))),
        TableId::Digital => Wavetable::from_cycles(16, |f, p| {
            let t = f as f32 / 15.0;
            let bits = (8.0 - 6.0 * t).round();
            let steps = (64.0 * 0.125f32.powf(t)).round();
            let v = 2.0 * (p * steps).floor() / steps - 1.0;
            let q = 2f32.powf(bits - 1.0) - 1.0;
            (v * q).round() / q
        }),
    }
}

/// Every table, in `TableId` order (a fresh build; tests time it).
pub fn build_all() -> Vec<Wavetable> {
    TableId::ALL.iter().map(|&id| build(id)).collect()
}

/// The shared tables, built on first use (call it once early, off the audio thread).
pub fn registry() -> &'static [Wavetable] {
    static REGISTRY: OnceLock<Vec<Wavetable>> = OnceLock::new();
    REGISTRY.get_or_init(build_all)
}

pub fn table(id: TableId) -> &'static Wavetable {
    &registry()[id as usize]
}
```

### Task 3: Oscillators, sub and noise

**Purpose.** Spec 3.2 (oscillator, unison, warp) and 3.4 (sub, noise). Pure per-sample kernels; the voice (Task 7) computes an `OscTick` once per control tick and calls `OscState::sample` for every sample.

**Files:**
- Create: `crates/lpd-core/src/synth/osc.rs`, `crates/lpd-core/tests/synth_osc.rs`
- Modify: `crates/lpd-core/src/synth/mod.rs` (`pub mod osc;`)

**Behaviour (from spec 3.2, exact):**
- Unison: `n` copies spaced evenly from −spread to +spread cents, spread = `100 × detune²`; the centre copy is index `(n − 1) / 2` (for an even count the inner copy below the note). Copy gains: centre 1, others BLEND, then all scaled so that Σ gain² = 1. Copy pans spread evenly across ±WIDTH and add to the oscillator PAN (clamped to ±1). Pan law: constant power, 1.0 on both sides at the centre (`cos θ · √2`, `sin θ · √2`, θ = (pan + 1)·π/4). The LEVEL knob gives gain LEVEL².
- Warp: SYNC reads at `frac(p × 16^amt)` and the band-limit level uses the frequency × that ratio; BEND reads at `p^(1 + 4·amt)`; PWM reads at `p / w` for `p < w` (w = 1 − 0.98·amt) and outputs 0 for the rest of the cycle; FM adds `amt × 8 / 2π × m` cycles to the phase (m = the other oscillator's centre copy, −1…1, i.e. index 0–8 rad); RM outputs `v + (v·m − v)·amt`.
- Phases: on a fresh note every copy starts at `PHASE RAND × random(0…1)`.

```rust
use lpd_core::audio::fft::fft_complex;
use lpd_core::synth::osc::*;
use lpd_core::synth::params::{NoiseColor, SubShape, TableId, Warp};
use lpd_core::synth::wavetable::table;

const RATE: f32 = 48_000.0;

fn rising_crossing_hz(x: &[f32]) -> f32 {
    let mut times = Vec::new();
    for i in 1..x.len() {
        if x[i - 1] < 0.0 && x[i] >= 0.0 {
            times.push((i - 1) as f32 + x[i - 1] / (x[i - 1] - x[i]));
        }
    }
    (times.len() - 1) as f32 / (times[times.len() - 1] - times[0]) * RATE
}

fn tick(hz: f32, pos: f32, unison: usize, warp: WarpShape) -> OscTick {
    OscTick::new(table(TableId::Basic), RATE, hz, pos, unison, 0.25, 0.75, 0.0, 0.0, 1.0, warp)
}

#[test]
fn a4_is_in_tune() {
    let t = tick(440.0, 0.0, 1, WarpShape::new(Warp::Off, 0.0));
    let mut s = OscState::default();
    let x: Vec<f32> = (0..48_000).map(|_| s.sample(&t, 0.0).0).collect();
    let hz = rising_crossing_hz(&x);
    assert!((hz - 440.0).abs() < 0.0254, "{hz}");
}

#[test]
fn unison_spreads_symmetrically() {
    let c = unison_cents(7, 0.5);
    let want = [-25.0, -16.6667, -8.3333, 0.0, 8.3333, 16.6667, 25.0];
    for (a, b) in c.iter().zip(want) {
        assert!((a - b).abs() < 1e-3, "{c:?}");
    }
    assert_eq!(unison_cents(1, 1.0)[0], 0.0);
    assert_eq!(&unison_cents(2, 1.0)[..2], &[-100.0, 100.0]);
    assert_eq!((centre(1), centre(2), centre(4), centre(7)), (0, 0, 1, 3));
}

#[test]
fn unison_gains_keep_unit_power() {
    for n in 1..=7 {
        for blend in [0.0, 0.5, 1.0] {
            let g = unison_gains(n, blend);
            let power: f32 = g.iter().map(|v| v * v).sum();
            assert!((power - 1.0).abs() < 1e-5, "n {n} blend {blend}");
        }
    }
    assert!((unison_gains(3, 0.75)[1] - 0.685_994).abs() < 1e-5);
    assert_eq!(&unison_pans(3, 1.0)[..3], &[-1.0, 0.0, 1.0]);
    assert_eq!(unison_pans(1, 1.0)[0], 0.0);
    let (l, r) = pan_gains(0.0);
    assert!((l - 1.0).abs() < 1e-6 && (r - 1.0).abs() < 1e-6);
}

#[test]
fn every_warp_stays_bounded() {
    for &mode in Warp::ALL {
        for amt in [0.0, 0.5, 1.0] {
            let t = tick(220.0, 0.667, 1, WarpShape::new(mode, amt));
            let mut s = OscState::default();
            for i in 0..4800 {
                let m = (std::f32::consts::TAU * 330.0 * i as f32 / RATE).sin();
                let (l, r) = s.sample(&t, m);
                assert!(l.abs() <= 1.5 && r.abs() <= 1.5, "{mode:?} {amt}: {l}");
            }
        }
    }
}

#[test]
fn warp_shapes_bend_the_phase() {
    let pwm = WarpShape::new(Warp::Pwm, 0.5);
    assert_eq!(pwm.phase(0.9), None);
    assert!((pwm.phase(0.2).unwrap() - 0.2 / 0.51).abs() < 1e-6);
    let sync = WarpShape::new(Warp::Sync, 0.25);
    assert!((sync.freq_factor() - 2.0).abs() < 1e-5);
    assert!((sync.phase(0.75).unwrap() - 0.5).abs() < 1e-5);
    assert!((WarpShape::new(Warp::Bend, 0.25).phase(0.5).unwrap() - 0.25).abs() < 1e-6);
    assert_eq!(WarpShape::new(Warp::Fm, 1.0).freq_factor(), 1.0);
}

#[test]
fn the_sub_plays_the_frequency_it_is_given() {
    for shape in [SubShape::Sine, SubShape::Square] {
        let mut s = SubState::default();
        let x: Vec<f32> = (0..48_000).map(|_| s.sample(shape, 110.0 / RATE)).collect();
        assert!((rising_crossing_hz(&x) - 110.0).abs() < 0.05, "{shape:?}");
    }
}

#[test]
fn pink_noise_falls_3_db_per_octave() {
    let mut n = NoiseState::new(7);
    let mut power = vec![0.0f64; 1024];
    for _ in 0..64 {
        let mut re: Vec<f32> = (0..2048)
            .map(|i| n.sample(NoiseColor::Pink) * (0.5 - 0.5 * (std::f32::consts::TAU * i as f32 / 2048.0).cos()))
            .collect();
        let mut im = vec![0.0; 2048];
        fft_complex(&mut re, &mut im, false);
        for k in 0..1024 {
            power[k] += f64::from(re[k] * re[k] + im[k] * im[k]);
        }
    }
    let band = |lo: usize, hi: usize| power[lo..=hi].iter().sum::<f64>() / (hi - lo + 1) as f64;
    // Bins of 23.4 Hz: 177–354 Hz and 2828–5657 Hz, four octaves apart.
    let ratio_db = 10.0 * (band(121, 241) / band(8, 15)).log10();
    assert!((-14.0..=-10.0).contains(&ratio_db), "{ratio_db}");
}

#[test]
fn white_noise_is_bounded_and_centred() {
    let mut n = NoiseState::new(3);
    let x: Vec<f32> = (0..48_000).map(|_| n.sample(NoiseColor::White)).collect();
    assert!(x.iter().all(|v| v.abs() <= 1.0));
    assert!((x.iter().sum::<f32>() / x.len() as f32).abs() < 0.01);
}
```

- [x] **Step 3: Implement** `crates/lpd-core/src/synth/osc.rs` (complete):

```rust
//! Wavetable oscillator with unison and warp, the sub oscillator and noise (spec §3.2, §3.4).

use super::params::{NoiseColor, SubShape, Warp, UNISON_MAX};
use super::wavetable::Wavetable;
use std::f32::consts::{FRAC_PI_4, SQRT_2, TAU};

/// xorshift32; the state must not be 0.
#[inline]
pub fn next_u32(state: &mut u32) -> u32 {
    let mut x = *state;
    x ^= x << 13;
    x ^= x >> 17;
    x ^= x << 5;
    *state = x;
    x
}

/// Uniform in [0, 1).
#[inline]
pub fn next_unit(state: &mut u32) -> f32 {
    (next_u32(state) >> 8) as f32 / (1u32 << 24) as f32
}

/// Uniform in [−1, 1).
#[inline]
pub fn next_bipolar(state: &mut u32) -> f32 {
    2.0 * next_unit(state) - 1.0
}

/// Band-limited step correction for naive saw and square waves.
#[inline]
pub fn polyblep(mut t: f32, dt: f32) -> f32 {
    if t < dt {
        t /= dt;
        t + t - t * t - 1.0
    } else if t > 1.0 - dt {
        t = (t - 1.0) / dt;
        t * t + t + t + 1.0
    } else {
        0.0
    }
}

/// Index of the centre copy (for an even count, the inner copy below the note).
pub fn centre(n: usize) -> usize {
    (n.clamp(1, UNISON_MAX) - 1) / 2
}

/// Pitch offsets of the copies in cents, spread evenly over ±100·detune².
pub fn unison_cents(n: usize, detune: f32) -> [f32; UNISON_MAX] {
    let n = n.clamp(1, UNISON_MAX);
    let spread = 100.0 * detune * detune;
    let mut out = [0.0; UNISON_MAX];
    if n > 1 {
        for (i, v) in out.iter_mut().enumerate().take(n) {
            *v = spread * (2.0 * i as f32 / (n - 1) as f32 - 1.0);
        }
    }
    out
}

/// Copy gains: the centre 1, the others BLEND, scaled to unit power.
pub fn unison_gains(n: usize, blend: f32) -> [f32; UNISON_MAX] {
    let n = n.clamp(1, UNISON_MAX);
    let c = centre(n);
    let mut g = [0.0; UNISON_MAX];
    let mut power = 0.0;
    for (i, v) in g.iter_mut().enumerate().take(n) {
        *v = if i == c { 1.0 } else { blend.clamp(0.0, 1.0) };
        power += *v * *v;
    }
    let norm = 1.0 / power.sqrt().max(1e-6);
    g.iter_mut().take(n).for_each(|v| *v *= norm);
    g
}

/// Copy pans −1…1 spread evenly across ±WIDTH (0 for a single copy).
pub fn unison_pans(n: usize, width: f32) -> [f32; UNISON_MAX] {
    let n = n.clamp(1, UNISON_MAX);
    let mut out = [0.0; UNISON_MAX];
    if n > 1 {
        for (i, v) in out.iter_mut().enumerate().take(n) {
            *v = width.clamp(0.0, 1.0) * (2.0 * i as f32 / (n - 1) as f32 - 1.0);
        }
    }
    out
}

/// Constant-power pan gains, 1.0 on both sides at the centre.
#[inline]
pub fn pan_gains(pan: f32) -> (f32, f32) {
    let a = (pan.clamp(-1.0, 1.0) + 1.0) * FRAC_PI_4;
    (a.cos() * SQRT_2, a.sin() * SQRT_2)
}

/// A warp mode with its per-tick constants.
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub struct WarpShape {
    pub mode: Warp,
    pub amt: f32,
    ratio: f32,
    exponent: f32,
    width: f32,
}

impl WarpShape {
    pub fn new(mode: Warp, amt: f32) -> WarpShape {
        let amt = amt.clamp(0.0, 1.0);
        WarpShape { mode, amt, ratio: 16f32.powf(amt), exponent: 1.0 + 4.0 * amt, width: 1.0 - 0.98 * amt }
    }

    /// The phase a frame is read at; `None` = the silent part of a PWM cycle.
    #[inline]
    pub fn phase(&self, p: f32) -> Option<f32> {
        match self.mode {
            Warp::Sync => Some((p * self.ratio).fract()),
            Warp::Bend => Some(p.powf(self.exponent)),
            Warp::Pwm => (p < self.width).then(|| p / self.width),
            Warp::Off | Warp::Fm | Warp::Rm => Some(p),
        }
    }

    /// Frequency multiplier for the band-limit choice (SYNC reads faster).
    pub fn freq_factor(&self) -> f32 {
        if self.mode == Warp::Sync {
            self.ratio
        } else {
            1.0
        }
    }
}

/// One control tick of oscillator settings (computed by the voice, read every sample).
#[derive(Clone, Copy)]
pub struct OscTick {
    pub table: &'static Wavetable,
    pub level: usize,
    pub pos: f32,
    pub n: usize,
    pub inc: [f32; UNISON_MAX],
    pub gain_l: [f32; UNISON_MAX],
    pub gain_r: [f32; UNISON_MAX],
    pub centre: usize,
    pub warp: WarpShape,
}

impl OscTick {
    /// `hz` is the note frequency with every pitch offset applied; `level` is the 0–1 knob.
    #[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
    pub fn new(
        table: &'static Wavetable,
        rate: f32,
        hz: f32,
        pos: f32,
        unison: usize,
        detune: f32,
        blend: f32,
        width: f32,
        pan: f32,
        level: f32,
        warp: WarpShape,
    ) -> OscTick {
        let n = unison.clamp(1, UNISON_MAX);
        let cents = unison_cents(n, detune);
        let gains = unison_gains(n, blend);
        let pans = unison_pans(n, width);
        let lvl = level.clamp(0.0, 1.0);
        let lvl = lvl * lvl;
        let mut t = OscTick {
            table,
            level: 0,
            pos,
            n,
            inc: [0.0; UNISON_MAX],
            gain_l: [0.0; UNISON_MAX],
            gain_r: [0.0; UNISON_MAX],
            centre: centre(n),
            warp,
        };
        let mut top = hz;
        for i in 0..n {
            let f = hz * (cents[i] / 1200.0).exp2();
            top = top.max(f);
            t.inc[i] = (f / rate).min(0.5);
            let (gl, gr) = pan_gains(pan + pans[i]);
            t.gain_l[i] = gains[i] * lvl * gl;
            t.gain_r[i] = gains[i] * lvl * gr;
        }
        t.level = Wavetable::level_for(top * warp.freq_factor(), rate);
        t
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Default)]
pub struct OscState {
    pub phase: [f32; UNISON_MAX],
    /// The centre copy's output at the previous sample (the other oscillator's FM / RM source).
    pub last_centre: f32,
}

impl OscState {
    /// A fresh note: every copy starts at PHASE RAND × random(0…1).
    pub fn reset(&mut self, phase_rand: f32, rng: &mut u32) {
        for p in &mut self.phase {
            *p = phase_rand.clamp(0.0, 1.0) * next_unit(rng);
        }
        self.last_centre = 0.0;
    }

    /// One stereo sample. `modulator` is the other oscillator's previous centre sample.
    #[inline]
    pub fn sample(&mut self, t: &OscTick, modulator: f32) -> (f32, f32) {
        let (mut l, mut r, mut centre_out) = (0.0, 0.0, 0.0);
        for i in 0..t.n {
            let mut p = self.phase[i];
            if t.warp.mode == Warp::Fm {
                p = (p + t.warp.amt * 8.0 / TAU * modulator).rem_euclid(1.0);
            }
            let mut v = match t.warp.phase(p) {
                Some(q) => t.table.read(t.level, t.pos, q),
                None => 0.0,
            };
            if t.warp.mode == Warp::Rm {
                v += (v * modulator - v) * t.warp.amt;
            }
            if i == t.centre {
                centre_out = v;
            }
            l += v * t.gain_l[i];
            r += v * t.gain_r[i];
            let next = self.phase[i] + t.inc[i];
            self.phase[i] = if next >= 1.0 { next - 1.0 } else { next };
        }
        self.last_centre = centre_out;
        (l, r)
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Default)]
pub struct SubState {
    pub phase: f32,
}

impl SubState {
    #[inline]
    pub fn sample(&mut self, shape: SubShape, inc: f32) -> f32 {
        let p = self.phase;
        let v = match shape {
            SubShape::Sine => (TAU * p).sin(),
            SubShape::Triangle => 1.0 - 4.0 * (p - 0.5).abs(),
            SubShape::Saw => 2.0 * p - 1.0 - polyblep(p, inc),
            SubShape::Square => {
                let raw = if p < 0.5 { 1.0 } else { -1.0 };
                raw + polyblep(p, inc) - polyblep((p + 0.5).fract(), inc)
            }
        };
        let next = p + inc;
        self.phase = if next >= 1.0 { next - 1.0 } else { next };
        v
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug)]
pub struct NoiseState {
    rng: u32,
    b: [f32; 3],
}

impl NoiseState {
    pub fn new(seed: u32) -> NoiseState {
        NoiseState { rng: seed.max(1), b: [0.0; 3] }
    }

    #[inline]
    pub fn sample(&mut self, color: NoiseColor) -> f32 {
        let white = next_bipolar(&mut self.rng);
        match color {
            NoiseColor::White => white,
            NoiseColor::Pink => {
                // Paul Kellet's economy pink filter (±0.5 dB above 10 Hz).
                self.b[0] = 0.997_65 * self.b[0] + white * 0.099_046;
                self.b[1] = 0.963 * self.b[1] + white * 0.296_516_4;
                self.b[2] = 0.57 * self.b[2] + white * 1.052_691_3;
                (self.b[0] + self.b[1] + self.b[2] + white * 0.1848) * 0.25
            }
        }
    }
}
```

### Task 4: Filters

**Purpose.** Spec 3.5 and K9: the Moog-style ladder (LP 24), the state-variable filter (LP 12, HP 12, BP 12, NOTCH), drive, and the analytic response the FILTER display draws.

**Files:**
- Create: `crates/lpd-core/src/synth/filter.rs`, `crates/lpd-core/tests/synth_filter.rs`
- Modify: `crates/lpd-core/src/synth/mod.rs` (`pub mod filter;`)

**Behaviour (exact):**
- `g = tan(π · cutoff / rate)` with cutoff clamped to 20 Hz … min(20 kHz, 0.45 × rate).
- Ladder: four TPT one-pole stages (`G = g / (1 + g)`), the feedback solved in closed form (`y4 = (G⁴x + S) / (1 + kG⁴)`, `S = (1 − G)(G³s₁ + G²s₂ + Gs₃ + s₄)`), the stage input `tanh(x − k·y4)`, `k = 4.1 × RESO` (self-oscillates at RESO 1), output × `√(1 + k)` (half of the resonance loss made up). The input is scaled by `LADDER_HEADROOM = 0.5` before the tanh and the output divided by it.
- SVF: Zavalishin TPT with `2R = 1/Q`, `Q = 0.5 × 50^RESO` (0.5 … 25). BP is scaled by `1/Q` (unity at the centre); NOTCH = `x − bp/Q`.
- DRIVE: pre-gain `dg = 10^(24·DRIVE/20)`. LP 24: the ladder gets `x·dg` (its own tanh saturates); the SVF types get `tanh(x·dg)` when DRIVE > 0 (no tanh at DRIVE 0). Both outputs × `1/√dg`.
- `response_db`: the analog prototype `1/((1 + s)⁴ + k) · √(1 + k)` for LP 24; `1/(s² + s/Q + 1)` × (1, s², s/Q, s² + 1) for LP 12 / HP 12 / BP 12 / NOTCH, at `s = j·f/cutoff`; plus `12 × DRIVE` dB (24 dB pre-gain minus 12 dB makeup); floor −120 dB.

```rust
use lpd_core::synth::filter::*;
use lpd_core::synth::params::FilterType;
use std::f32::consts::PI;

const RATE: f32 = 48_000.0;
const ALL: [FilterType; 5] = [FilterType::Lp24, FilterType::Lp12, FilterType::Hp12, FilterType::Bp12, FilterType::Notch];

fn res(kind: FilterType, reso: f32) -> f32 {
    if kind == FilterType::Lp24 {
        ladder_k(reso)
    } else {
        svf_q(reso)
    }
}

/// Gain in dB of a small sine at `hz` through the filter (steady state).
fn measured_db(kind: FilterType, cutoff: f32, reso: f32, hz: f32) -> f32 {
    let mut f = VoiceFilter::default();
    f.set_kind(kind);
    let g = g_for(cutoff, RATE);
    let (mut sum_in, mut sum_out) = (0.0f64, 0.0f64);
    for i in 0..28_800 {
        let x = 0.01 * (2.0 * PI * hz * i as f32 / RATE).sin();
        let (y, _) = f.process(x, x, g, res(kind, reso), 1.0);
        if i >= 14_400 {
            sum_in += f64::from(x * x);
            sum_out += f64::from(y * y);
        }
    }
    (10.0 * (sum_out / sum_in).log10()) as f32
}

#[test]
fn each_type_matches_its_analytic_curve() {
    let fc = 1_000.0;
    for kind in ALL {
        for hz in [100.0, 300.0, 700.0, 1_000.0, 1_500.0, 3_000.0, 6_000.0] {
            // The bilinear transform maps `hz` onto this analog frequency.
            let analog = fc * (PI * hz / RATE).tan() / (PI * fc / RATE).tan();
            let want = response_db(kind, fc, 0.3, 0.0, &[analog])[0];
            if want < -40.0 {
                continue;
            }
            let got = measured_db(kind, fc, 0.3, hz);
            assert!((got - want).abs() <= 1.0, "{kind:?} at {hz} Hz: {got:.2} dB vs {want:.2} dB");
        }
    }
}

#[test]
fn the_ladder_self_oscillates_at_full_resonance() {
    let mut l = Ladder::default();
    let (g, k) = (g_for(1_000.0, RATE), ladder_k(1.0));
    let mut out = Vec::with_capacity(28_800);
    for i in 0..28_800 {
        out.push(l.process(if i == 0 { 0.5 } else { 0.0 }, g, k));
    }
    let tail = &out[24_000..];
    let rms = (tail.iter().map(|v| v * v).sum::<f32>() / tail.len() as f32).sqrt();
    assert!(rms > 0.05, "rms {rms}");
    let crossings = tail.windows(2).filter(|w| w[0] < 0.0 && w[1] >= 0.0).count() as f32;
    let hz = crossings / (tail.len() as f32 / RATE);
    assert!((hz - 1_000.0).abs() < 50.0, "{hz}");
}

#[test]
fn fast_cutoff_modulation_stays_finite() {
    let mut rng = 1u32;
    for kind in ALL {
        let mut f = VoiceFilter::default();
        f.set_kind(kind);
        for i in 0..48_000 {
            let cutoff = 50.0 * 300f32.powf(0.5 + 0.5 * (2.0 * PI * 2_000.0 * i as f32 / RATE).sin());
            let x = 0.5 * lpd_core::synth::osc::next_bipolar(&mut rng);
            let (y, _) = f.process(x, x, g_for(cutoff, RATE), res(kind, 0.9), 1.0);
            assert!(y.is_finite() && y.abs() < 10.0, "{kind:?}: {y}");
        }
    }
}

#[test]
fn drive_shifts_the_drawn_level() {
    let base = response_db(FilterType::Lp12, 1_000.0, 0.0, 0.0, &[100.0])[0];
    let driven = response_db(FilterType::Lp12, 1_000.0, 0.0, 0.5, &[100.0])[0];
    assert!((driven - base - 6.0).abs() < 1e-3);
}

#[test]
fn resonance_ranges() {
    assert_eq!(svf_q(0.0), 0.5);
    assert!((svf_q(1.0) - 25.0).abs() < 1e-4);
    assert!((ladder_k(1.0) - 4.1).abs() < 1e-6);
    assert_eq!(drive_gain(0.0), 1.0);
    assert!((drive_gain(1.0) - 15.848_93).abs() < 1e-3);
    assert!((g_for(30_000.0, RATE) - (PI * 20_000.0 / RATE).tan()).abs() < 1e-6, "clamped to 20 kHz");
}
```

```rust
//! Voice filters (spec §3.5, plan K9): a Moog-style ladder for LP 24 and a topology-preserving
//! state-variable filter for LP 12, HP 12, BP 12 and NOTCH. Two instances per voice (stereo).

use super::params::FilterType;
use std::f32::consts::PI;

/// The ladder's input is scaled by this before its saturating stage (and the output back), so
/// ordinary levels stay clean and loud ones saturate like the original.
pub const LADDER_HEADROOM: f32 = 0.5;

/// Prewarped integrator gain for `cutoff` (clamped to 20 Hz … min(20 kHz, 0.45 × rate)).
#[inline]
pub fn g_for(cutoff: f32, rate: f32) -> f32 {
    let top = 20_000.0f32.min(0.45 * rate);
    (PI * cutoff.clamp(20.0, top) / rate).tan()
}

pub fn ladder_k(reso: f32) -> f32 {
    4.1 * reso.clamp(0.0, 1.0)
}

pub fn svf_q(reso: f32) -> f32 {
    0.5 * 50f32.powf(reso.clamp(0.0, 1.0))
}

/// DRIVE 0–1 → pre-gain 0–24 dB.
pub fn drive_gain(drive: f32) -> f32 {
    10f32.powf(24.0 * drive.clamp(0.0, 1.0) / 20.0)
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Default)]
pub struct Ladder {
    s: [f32; 4],
}

impl Ladder {
    #[inline]
    pub fn process(&mut self, x: f32, g: f32, k: f32) -> f32 {
        let x = x * LADDER_HEADROOM;
        let gg = g / (1.0 + g);
        let g2 = gg * gg;
        let g4 = g2 * g2;
        let sum = (1.0 - gg) * (gg * g2 * self.s[0] + g2 * self.s[1] + gg * self.s[2] + self.s[3]);
        let y4 = (g4 * x + sum) / (1.0 + k * g4);
        let mut input = (x - k * y4).tanh();
        for s in &mut self.s {
            let v = (input - *s) * gg;
            let y = v + *s;
            *s = y + v;
            input = y;
        }
        input * (1.0 + k).sqrt() / LADDER_HEADROOM
    }

    pub fn reset(&mut self) {
        self.s = [0.0; 4];
    }

    pub fn is_finite(&self) -> bool {
        self.s.iter().all(|v| v.is_finite())
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Default)]
pub struct Svf {
    s1: f32,
    s2: f32,
}

impl Svf {
    #[inline]
    pub fn process(&mut self, x: f32, g: f32, q: f32, kind: FilterType) -> f32 {
        let r2 = 1.0 / q;
        let hp = (x - (r2 + g) * self.s1 - self.s2) / (1.0 + r2 * g + g * g);
        let bp = g * hp + self.s1;
        self.s1 = g * hp + bp;
        let lp = g * bp + self.s2;
        self.s2 = g * bp + lp;
        match kind {
            FilterType::Hp12 => hp,
            FilterType::Bp12 => bp * r2,
            FilterType::Notch => x - r2 * bp,
            FilterType::Lp12 | FilterType::Lp24 => lp,
        }
    }

    pub fn reset(&mut self) {
        self.s1 = 0.0;
        self.s2 = 0.0;
    }

    pub fn is_finite(&self) -> bool {
        self.s1.is_finite() && self.s2.is_finite()
    }
}

/// A voice's stereo filter. The voice passes coefficients once per control tick.
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Default)]
pub struct VoiceFilter {
    ladder: [Ladder; 2],
    svf: [Svf; 2],
    kind: FilterType,
}

impl VoiceFilter {
    /// A new type starts from silence (the other topology's state means nothing to it).
    pub fn set_kind(&mut self, kind: FilterType) {
        if kind != self.kind {
            self.reset();
            self.kind = kind;
        }
    }

    pub fn kind(&self) -> FilterType {
        self.kind
    }

    pub fn reset(&mut self) {
        self.ladder = [Ladder::default(); 2];
        self.svf = [Svf::default(); 2];
    }

    /// `res` = `ladder_k(reso)` for LP 24, else `svf_q(reso)`; `dg` = `drive_gain(drive)`.
    #[inline]
    pub fn process(&mut self, l: f32, r: f32, g: f32, res: f32, dg: f32) -> (f32, f32) {
        let makeup = 1.0 / dg.sqrt();
        match self.kind {
            FilterType::Lp24 => (
                self.ladder[0].process(l * dg, g, res) * makeup,
                self.ladder[1].process(r * dg, g, res) * makeup,
            ),
            kind => {
                let (l, r) = if dg > 1.0001 { ((l * dg).tanh(), (r * dg).tanh()) } else { (l, r) };
                (self.svf[0].process(l, g, res, kind) * makeup, self.svf[1].process(r, g, res, kind) * makeup)
            }
        }
    }

    pub fn is_finite(&self) -> bool {
        self.ladder.iter().all(Ladder::is_finite) && self.svf.iter().all(Svf::is_finite)
    }
}

/// Linear magnitude response in dB at `freqs` (analog prototype); DRIVE shifts it by 12·DRIVE dB.
/// For the telemetry thread (allocates).
pub fn response_db(kind: FilterType, cutoff: f32, reso: f32, drive: f32, freqs: &[f32]) -> Vec<f32> {
    let offset = 12.0 * drive.clamp(0.0, 1.0);
    freqs
        .iter()
        .map(|&f| {
            let w = f / cutoff.max(1.0);
            let mag = match kind {
                FilterType::Lp24 => {
                    let k = ladder_k(reso);
                    let a = 1.0 - w * w;
                    (1.0 + k).sqrt() / (a * a - 4.0 * w * w + k).hypot(4.0 * w * a)
                }
                FilterType::Lp12 | FilterType::Hp12 | FilterType::Bp12 | FilterType::Notch => {
                    let q = svf_q(reso);
                    let den = ((1.0 - w * w).powi(2) + (w / q).powi(2)).sqrt();
                    match kind {
                        FilterType::Hp12 => w * w / den,
                        FilterType::Bp12 => (w / q) / den,
                        FilterType::Notch => (1.0 - w * w).abs() / den,
                        _ => 1.0 / den,
                    }
                }
            };
            (20.0 * mag.max(1e-6).log10() + offset).max(-120.0)
        })
        .collect()
}
```
