```rust
//! The FX chain after the voices (spec 5): DRIVE → CHORUS → DELAY → REVERB.

mod chorus;
mod delay;
mod drive;
mod line;
mod reverb;

pub use chorus::Chorus;
pub use delay::Delay;
pub use drive::Drive;
pub use reverb::Reverb;

use crate::synth::params::FxParams;

const QUIET_IN: f32 = 1e-6;
/// −100 dB.
const QUIET_TAIL: f32 = 1e-5;

fn peak(l: &[f32], r: &[f32]) -> f32 {
    l.iter().chain(r).fold(0.0, |m, v| m.max(v.abs()))
}

pub struct FxChain {
    pub drive: Drive,
    pub chorus: Chorus,
    pub delay: Delay,
    pub reverb: Reverb,
    was_on: [bool; 4],
    tail: [f32; 4],
    idle: [bool; 4],
}

impl FxChain {
    pub fn new(rate: f32) -> FxChain {
        FxChain {
            drive: Drive::new(rate),
            chorus: Chorus::new(rate),
            delay: Delay::new(rate),
            reverb: Reverb::new(rate),
            was_on: [false; 4],
            tail: [0.0; 4],
            idle: [false; 4],
        }
    }

    pub fn clear(&mut self) {
        self.drive.clear();
        self.chorus.clear();
        self.delay.clear();
        self.reverb.clear();
        self.tail = [0.0; 4];
    }

    /// Units skipped in the last block (DRIVE, CHORUS, DELAY, REVERB).
    pub fn idle(&self) -> [bool; 4] {
        self.idle
    }

    pub fn process(&mut self, l: &mut [f32], r: &mut [f32], p: &FxParams, bpm: f32) {
        let on = [p.drive.on, p.chorus.on, p.delay.on, p.reverb.on];
        for unit in 0..4 {
            if !on[unit] {
                self.was_on[unit] = false;
                self.idle[unit] = false;
                continue;
            }
            if !self.was_on[unit] {
                match unit {
                    0 => self.drive.clear(),
                    1 => self.chorus.clear(),
                    2 => self.delay.clear(),
                    _ => self.reverb.clear(),
                }
                self.was_on[unit] = true;
                self.tail[unit] = 0.0;
            }
            if peak(l, r) < QUIET_IN && self.tail[unit] < QUIET_TAIL {
                self.idle[unit] = true;
                continue;
            }
            self.idle[unit] = false;
            match unit {
                0 => self.drive.process(l, r, &p.drive),
                1 => self.chorus.process(l, r, &p.chorus),
                2 => self.delay.process(l, r, &p.delay, bpm),
                _ => self.reverb.process(l, r, &p.reverb),
            }
            self.tail[unit] = peak(l, r);
        }
    }
}
```

Add the performance test of spec 11 now, so the budget is known early (risk, spec 14) — `crates/lpd-core/tests/synth_perf.rs`:

```rust
use lpd_core::instrument::Instrument;
use lpd_core::synth::params::*;
use lpd_core::synth::Synth;

/// Worst case (spec 11): 16 notes, both oscillators at unison 7, LP 24 with drive, all four FX.
#[test]
#[cfg_attr(debug_assertions, ignore)]
fn the_synth_meets_its_cpu_budget() {
    let mut p = SynthParams::default();
    p.osc_a.unison = 7;
    p.osc_b = OscParams { on: true, unison: 7, ..OscParams::default() };
    p.sub.on = true;
    p.noise.on = true;
    p.filter.drive = 0.5;
    p.fx.drive.on = true;
    p.fx.chorus.on = true;
    p.fx.delay.on = true;
    p.fx.reverb.on = true;
    let mut s = Synth::new(48_000);
    s.load_params(&p);
    let (mut l, mut r) = ([0.0f32; 128], [0.0f32; 128]);
    s.render_add(&mut l, &mut r);
    s.render_add(&mut l, &mut r);
    for n in 0..16 {
        s.note_on(48 + n * 2, 100);
    }
    let blocks = 48_000 * 10 / 128;
    let t0 = std::time::Instant::now();
    for _ in 0..blocks {
        s.render_add(&mut l, &mut r);
    }
    let elapsed = t0.elapsed().as_secs_f32();
    eprintln!("10 s of worst-case synth audio rendered in {elapsed:.3} s");
    assert!(elapsed < 2.5, "{elapsed} s is over 25 % of real time");
}
```

## Phase B — Integration

Task 8 verification (2026-10-24): Prescribed tests written first; synth_fx failed to compile on the absent fx module. Supplied DSP then exposed three failures: silent gaps froze echo/reverb buffers. User decision K22 added constant-time pending-sample tracking and checks of FIR/feedback states before sleeping. Nine tests then passed; the remaining ping-pong threshold contradicted the prescribed feedback low-pass (actual/analytic 0.272031), corrected under prompt rule 2 and recorded in K23. All 10 synth_fx tests now PASS. `cargo test -p lpd-core` PASS; `cargo fmt --all -- --check` PASS; `cargo test --workspace` PASS (483 passed, 0 failed, 2 ignored); `npm test` PASS (127 passed); `npm run check` PASS (0 errors, 0 warnings). Named release CPU test PASS: 10 s of worst-case synth audio rendered in 1.315 s (13.15% of real time, budget <2.5 s), per K21 release-command correction. Two inline reviews checked stereo DSP/order, wet/dry mixes, buffer sizing, feedback, clearing and fixed-state updates without allocation/free/lock/I/O. Existing echo, decay, idle/wake and clear tests cover K22; engine allocation enforcement remains Task 10. Other deviations: rustfmt only. No app/preview/listening/hardware checks yet.

**Contracts (Rust):**

```rust
// synth/patch.rs
pub const PATCH_VERSION: u32 = 1;
pub const NAME_MAX: usize = 24;
#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize, Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Default)] #[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub enum Category { Piano, #[default] Keys, Organ, Pad, Lead, Bass, Pluck, Fx }
impl Category {
    pub const ALL: [Category; 8];
    pub fn id(self) -> &'static str;      // "piano" … "fx"
    pub fn label(self) -> &'static str;   // "PIANO" … "FX"
    pub fn color(self) -> [u8; 3];        // spec 6.5 LED triples
}
#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize, Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)] #[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub enum PatchSource { Factory(String), User(String), Init }     // {"factory":"ePiano"} | {"user":"X.lbpatch"} | "init"
#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize, Clone, Debug, PartialEq)] #[serde(rename_all = "camelCase", default)]
pub struct Patch { pub patch_version: u32, pub name: String, pub category: Category, pub params: SynthParams }
impl Patch {
    pub fn new(name: &str, category: Category, params: SynthParams) -> Patch;   // cleans the name
    pub fn init() -> Patch;                                                      // "INIT", Keys, SynthParams::default()
    pub fn from_json(s: &str) -> anyhow::Result<Patch>;  // newer patchVersion → Err("Patch made by a newer version"); clamps; cleans the name
    pub fn to_json(&self) -> String;                       // pretty JSON
}
impl Default for Patch { fn default() -> Patch { Patch::init() } }
pub fn clamped(p: SynthParams) -> SynthParams;  // every ParamId re-set through its kind
pub fn clean_name(name: &str) -> String;        // trim, uppercase, ≤ 24 chars, "" → "UNTITLED"
pub fn file_name(name: &str) -> String;         // "<NAME>.lbpatch", Windows-safe

// synth/factory.rs
pub const FACTORY_IDS: [&str; 16];
pub fn factory(id: &str) -> Option<Patch>;
pub fn all() -> Vec<Patch>;

// synth/export.rs
pub fn ui_meta() -> serde_json::Value;
pub fn ui_meta_ts() -> String;

// instrument/mod.rs (replaces the old KeysSettings)
#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize, Clone, Debug, PartialEq)] #[serde(rename_all = "camelCase", default)]
pub struct KeysSettings { pub patch: Patch, pub patch_source: PatchSource, pub modified: bool, pub gm_sound: KeysSound, pub volume_db: f32, pub output: Option<u8> }
// Default: factory("ePiano"), PatchSource::Factory("ePiano"), false, KeysSound::EPiano, −6.0, None
#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize, Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq)] #[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub struct KeysMix { pub gm_sound: KeysSound, pub volume_db: f32, pub output: Option<u8> }
pub fn factory_id(sound: KeysSound) -> &'static str;   // Piano → "piano", EPiano → "ePiano", … SynthBass → "synthBass"

// devices/lcxl.rs
pub const SYNTH_KNOBS: [[ParamId; 8]; 3];   // spec 10.2 table, row 0 = top
pub fn knob_led_index(row: u8, ch: u8) -> u8; // row × 8 + ch
```

**Category table:** `Piano` "piano" "PIANO" [63,63,63] · `Keys` "keys" "KEYS" [63,40,0] · `Organ` "organ" "ORGAN" [63,20,0] · `Pad` "pad" "PAD" [25,0,63] · `Lead` "lead" "LEAD" [0,63,25] · `Bass` "bass" "BASS" [0,30,63] · `Pluck` "pluck" "PLUCK" [63,63,0] · `Fx` "fx" "FX" [63,0,40].

**`file_name`:** clean the name; replace control characters and `< > : " / \ | ? *` with `_`; trim trailing dots and spaces; an empty result becomes `UNTITLED`; the reserved Windows names `CON PRN AUX NUL COM1…COM9 LPT1…LPT9` get a trailing `_`; append `.lbpatch`.

**Set migration** (`Set::from_json`): parse to `serde_json::Value`; when `version` is 1, replace `keys` with `{ patch: factory(factory_id(sound)), patchSource: { factory: id }, modified: false, gmSound: sound, volumeDb, output }` (missing `keys` or `sound` → `ePiano`; missing `volumeDb` → −6; missing `output` → null) and set `version` to 2; then deserialize and check `version == SET_VERSION` and the shape, as today.

**`ui_meta()` JSON** (the `SynthMeta` type in `synth-types.ts`):

```json
{
  "params": [{ "id": "oscA.pos", "section": "OSC A", "label": "POS", "kind": "float", "min": 0.0, "max": 1.0,
               "taper": "lin", "unit": "pct", "default": 0.667, "dest": "oscA.pos" }, …],
  "dests": [{ "id": "oscA.pos", "label": "OSC A · POS", "param": "oscA.pos" }, …],
  "sources": [{ "id": "env1", "label": "ENV1", "color": "#ff5b1f" }, …],
  "categories": [{ "id": "piano", "label": "PIANO", "color": [63, 63, 63] }, …],
  "lcxl": [["oscA.pos", …8], […], […]],
  "init": { …SynthParams::default()… },
  "factory": [{ "id": "piano", "name": "PIANO", "category": "piano", "params": { … } }, …]
}
```

Per kind: `bool` → no min/max/unit; `choice` → `"choices": [ids]`, `"labels": [labels]`; `int` → `min`, `max`, `unit`; `float` → `min`, `max`, `taper` (`"lin" | "log" | "cubic"`), `unit` (`"pct" "hz" "sec" "cents" "semis" "db" "pan" "deg" "envOct" "drive24" "drive36" "oct" "voices"`). `default` = `id.get(&SynthParams::default())`. `dest` only when a `Dest` names the param. `ui_meta_ts()` returns:

```ts
// Generated by `cargo test -p lpd-core --test synth_meta` from crates/lpd-core/src/synth. Do not edit.
import type { SynthMeta } from './synth-types.ts';

export const SYNTH_META: SynthMeta = <ui_meta() as pretty JSON>;
```

**Factory recipes** (`factory.rs`; built from `SynthParams::default()`; `vibrato(p, lfo)` sets that LFO to a 5.5 Hz TRIG sine and pushes `LFOn → osc.pitch` 0.021 with AUX WHEEL; BASIC positions: sine 0, triangle 1/3, saw 2/3, square 1):

```rust
//! The factory patches (spec 8.3). Recipes are starting points; Task 19 tunes them by ear.

use super::params::*;
use super::patch::{Category, Patch};

pub const FACTORY_IDS: [&str; 16] = [
    "piano", "ePiano", "organ", "synthPad", "synthLead", "synthBass", "moogBass", "acidBass",
    "wobbleBass", "supersaw", "syncLead", "pluck", "brass", "strings", "vowelPad", "fmBell",
];

const SINE: f32 = 0.0;
const TRIANGLE: f32 = 1.0 / 3.0;
const SAW: f32 = 2.0 / 3.0;
const SQUARE: f32 = 1.0;

fn route(source: Source, dest: Dest, amount: f32) -> Route {
    Route { source, dest: Some(dest), amount, aux: None, bypass: false }
}

fn vibrato(p: &mut SynthParams, lfo: usize) {
    p.lfo[lfo] = LfoParams { shape: LfoShape::Sine, rate: 5.5, mode: LfoMode::Trig, ..LfoParams::default() };
    let source = if lfo == 0 { Source::Lfo1 } else { Source::Lfo2 };
    p.routes.push(Route { aux: Some(Source::Wheel), ..route(source, Dest::OscPitch, 0.021) });
}

fn env(attack: f32, decay: f32, sustain: f32, release: f32) -> EnvParams {
    EnvParams { attack, decay, sustain, release }
}

fn osc(table: TableId, pos: f32) -> OscParams {
    OscParams { table, pos, ..OscParams::default() }
}

fn second(table: TableId, pos: f32) -> OscParams {
    OscParams { on: true, ..osc(table, pos) }
}

fn filter(kind: FilterType, cutoff: f32) -> FilterParams {
    FilterParams { kind, cutoff, ..FilterParams::default() }
}

pub fn factory(id: &str) -> Option<Patch> {
    use TableId::{Basic, Harmonics, Organ, Pwm, Vowels};
    let mut p = SynthParams::default();
    let mut lfo_for_vibrato = 0;
    let (name, category) = match id {
        "piano" => {
            p.osc_a = osc(Harmonics, 0.15);
            p.osc_b = OscParams { oct: 1, level: 0.3, ..second(Basic, TRIANGLE) };
            p.filter = FilterParams { key_track: 0.5, env_amt: 0.3, ..filter(FilterType::Lp12, 3_000.0) };
            p.env[0] = env(0.002, 1.2, 0.0, 0.25);
            p.env[1] = env(0.001, 0.8, 0.0, 0.2);
            p.routes.push(route(Source::Vel, Dest::Cutoff, 0.2));
            p.fx.reverb = ReverbFx { on: true, mix: 0.15, ..ReverbFx::default() };
            ("PIANO", Category::Piano)
        }
        "ePiano" => {
            p.osc_a = OscParams { warp: Warp::Fm, warp_amt: 0.1, ..osc(Basic, SINE) };
            p.osc_b = OscParams { oct: 1, level: 0.0, ..second(Basic, SINE) };
            p.env[2] = env(0.001, 0.4, 0.0, 0.15);
            p.routes.push(route(Source::Env3, Dest::OscAWarp, 0.3));
            p.env[0] = env(0.002, 1.5, 0.3, 0.4);
            p.fx.chorus = ChorusFx { on: true, mix: 0.3, ..ChorusFx::default() };
            ("E.PIANO", Category::Keys)
        }
        "organ" => {
            p.osc_a = osc(Organ, 0.5);
            p.filter.on = false;
            p.env[0] = env(0.005, 0.0, 1.0, 0.005);
            p.fx.chorus = ChorusFx { on: true, rate: 5.5, depth: 0.7, mix: 0.4, ..ChorusFx::default() };
            ("ORGAN", Category::Organ)
        }
        "synthPad" => {
            p.osc_a = OscParams { unison: 3, detune: 0.26, width: 0.8, ..osc(Basic, SAW) };
            p.filter = filter(FilterType::Lp12, 2_500.0);
            p.env[0] = env(0.3, 0.0, 1.0, 1.2);
            p.lfo[1] = LfoParams { shape: LfoShape::Triangle, rate: 0.3, mode: LfoMode::Free, ..LfoParams::default() };
            p.routes.push(route(Source::Lfo2, Dest::Cutoff, 0.1));
            p.fx.chorus = ChorusFx { on: true, mix: 0.3, ..ChorusFx::default() };
            p.fx.reverb = ReverbFx { on: true, mix: 0.35, decay: 4.0, ..ReverbFx::default() };
            ("SYNTH PAD", Category::Pad)
        }
        "synthLead" => {
            p.osc_a = osc(Basic, SQUARE);
            p.osc_b = OscParams { level: 0.55, ..second(Basic, SAW) };
            p.filter = FilterParams { reso: 0.2, ..filter(FilterType::Lp24, 4_000.0) };
            p.env[0] = env(0.005, 0.2, 0.7, 0.15);
            p.voice = VoiceParams { mode: VoiceMode::Legato, glide: 0.04, ..VoiceParams::default() };
            p.fx.delay = DelayFx { on: true, sync: true, sync_div: SyncDiv::D1_8D, mix: 0.2, ..DelayFx::default() };
            ("SYNTH LEAD", Category::Lead)
        }
        "synthBass" => {
            p.osc_a = OscParams { oct: -1, ..osc(Basic, SAW) };
            p.filter = filter(FilterType::Lp24, 800.0);
            p.env[0] = env(0.003, 0.3, 0.6, 0.08);
            ("SYNTH BASS", Category::Bass)
        }
        "moogBass" => {
            p.osc_a = osc(Basic, SAW);
            p.osc_b = OscParams { oct: -1, fine: -7.0, ..second(Basic, SQUARE) };
            p.sub = SubParams { on: true, level: 0.5, ..SubParams::default() };
            p.filter = FilterParams { reso: 0.3, drive: 0.4, env_amt: 0.45, ..filter(FilterType::Lp24, 300.0) };
            p.env[1] = env(0.0, 0.35, 0.0, 0.12);
            p.voice = VoiceParams { mode: VoiceMode::Mono, glide: 0.06, glide_mode: GlideMode::Legato, drift: 0.1, ..VoiceParams::default() };
            ("MOOG BASS", Category::Bass)
        }
        "acidBass" => {
            p.osc_a = osc(Basic, SAW);
            p.filter = FilterParams { reso: 0.75, drive: 0.3, env_amt: 0.6, ..filter(FilterType::Lp24, 400.0) };
            p.env[1] = env(0.001, 0.2, 0.0, 0.1);
            p.routes.push(route(Source::Vel, Dest::Cutoff, 0.25));
            p.voice = VoiceParams { mode: VoiceMode::Mono, glide: 0.08, glide_mode: GlideMode::Legato, ..VoiceParams::default() };
            p.fx.drive = DriveFx { on: true, kind: DriveType::Tube, amount: 0.3, ..DriveFx::default() };
            ("ACID BASS", Category::Bass)
        }
        "wobbleBass" => {
            p.osc_a = OscParams { unison: 3, detune: 0.2, ..osc(Basic, SAW) };
            p.osc_b = OscParams { oct: -1, ..second(Basic, SQUARE) };
            p.filter = FilterParams { reso: 0.4, ..filter(FilterType::Lp24, 250.0) };
            p.lfo[0] = LfoParams { sync: true, sync_div: SyncDiv::D1_8, ..LfoParams::default() };
            p.routes.push(route(Source::Lfo1, Dest::Cutoff, 0.55));
            p.fx.drive = DriveFx { on: true, kind: DriveType::Fold, amount: 0.25, ..DriveFx::default() };
            lfo_for_vibrato = 1;
            ("WOBBLE BASS", Category::Bass)
        }
        "supersaw" => {
            p.osc_a = OscParams { unison: 7, detune: 0.45, width: 1.0, blend: 0.8, ..osc(Basic, SAW) };
            p.osc_b = OscParams { oct: 1, unison: 7, detune: 0.3, level: 0.5, ..second(Basic, SAW) };
            p.filter = filter(FilterType::Lp12, 6_000.0);
            p.env[0] = env(0.005, 0.0, 1.0, 0.3);
            p.fx.delay = DelayFx { on: true, sync: true, sync_div: SyncDiv::D1_4, mix: 0.18, ..DelayFx::default() };
            p.fx.reverb = ReverbFx { on: true, mix: 0.25, ..ReverbFx::default() };
            ("SUPERSAW", Category::Lead)
        }
        "syncLead" => {
            p.osc_a = OscParams { warp: Warp::Sync, warp_amt: 0.3, ..osc(Basic, SAW) };
            p.routes.push(route(Source::Env2, Dest::OscAWarp, 0.4));
            p.osc_b = OscParams { oct: -1, level: 0.4, ..second(Basic, SQUARE) };
            p.filter = filter(FilterType::Lp24, 5_000.0);
            p.voice = VoiceParams { mode: VoiceMode::Mono, glide: 0.03, ..VoiceParams::default() };
            p.fx.delay = DelayFx { on: true, mix: 0.15, ..DelayFx::default() };
            ("SYNC LEAD", Category::Lead)
        }
        "pluck" => {
            p.osc_a = OscParams { unison: 3, detune: 0.15, ..osc(Basic, SAW) };
            p.osc_b = OscParams { level: 0.4, ..second(Harmonics, 0.6) };
            p.filter = FilterParams { env_amt: 0.55, ..filter(FilterType::Lp24, 600.0) };
            p.env[0] = env(0.001, 0.45, 0.0, 0.3);
            p.env[1] = env(0.001, 0.25, 0.0, 0.2);
            p.fx.reverb = ReverbFx { on: true, mix: 0.2, ..ReverbFx::default() };
            ("PLUCK", Category::Pluck)
        }
        "brass" => {
            p.osc_a = osc(Basic, SAW);
            p.osc_b = OscParams { fine: 8.0, ..second(Basic, SAW) };
            p.filter = FilterParams { env_amt: 0.35, ..filter(FilterType::Lp24, 900.0) };
            p.env[1] = env(0.08, 0.4, 0.4, 0.2);
            p.env[0] = env(0.04, 0.0, 0.9, 0.2);
            p.fx.reverb = ReverbFx { on: true, mix: 0.15, ..ReverbFx::default() };
            ("BRASS", Category::Lead)
        }
        "strings" => {
            p.osc_a = OscParams { unison: 5, detune: 0.22, width: 0.9, ..osc(Basic, SAW) };
            p.osc_b = OscParams { unison: 3, ..second(Pwm, 0.3) };
            p.filter = filter(FilterType::Lp12, 3_500.0);
            p.env[0] = env(0.45, 0.0, 1.0, 0.9);
            p.fx.chorus = ChorusFx { on: true, mix: 0.4, ..ChorusFx::default() };
            p.fx.reverb = ReverbFx { on: true, mix: 0.35, ..ReverbFx::default() };
            ("STRINGS", Category::Pad)
        }
        "vowelPad" => {
            p.osc_a = OscParams { unison: 3, ..osc(Vowels, 0.5) };
            p.lfo[1] = LfoParams { shape: LfoShape::Triangle, rate: 0.15, mode: LfoMode::Free, ..LfoParams::default() };
            p.routes.push(route(Source::Lfo2, Dest::OscAPos, 0.5));
            p.filter = filter(FilterType::Lp12, 4_000.0);
            p.env[0] = env(0.6, 0.0, 1.0, 1.5);
            p.fx.reverb = ReverbFx { on: true, mix: 0.45, ..ReverbFx::default() };
            ("VOWEL PAD", Category::Pad)
        }
        "fmBell" => {
            p.osc_a = OscParams { warp: Warp::Fm, warp_amt: 0.15, ..osc(Basic, SINE) };
            p.osc_b = OscParams { oct: 1, semi: 9, fine: 69.0, level: 0.0, ..second(Basic, SINE) };
            p.env[2] = env(0.001, 1.5, 0.0, 0.5);
            p.routes.push(route(Source::Env3, Dest::OscAWarp, 0.45));
            p.env[0] = env(0.001, 2.5, 0.0, 1.5);
            p.fx.reverb = ReverbFx { on: true, mix: 0.3, ..ReverbFx::default() };
            ("FM BELL", Category::Keys)
        }
        _ => return None,
    };
    vibrato(&mut p, lfo_for_vibrato);
    Some(Patch::new(name, category, p))
}

pub fn all() -> Vec<Patch> {
    FACTORY_IDS.iter().filter_map(|id| factory(id)).collect()
}
```

Note: `vibrato` overwrites the chosen LFO's settings — the recipes above never use that LFO for anything else (WOBBLE BASS uses LFO1, so its vibrato goes on LFO2; SYNTH PAD and VOWEL PAD use LFO2, so theirs stays on LFO1).

`crates/lpd-core/tests/synth_patch.rs`:

```rust
use lpd_core::instrument::Instrument;
use lpd_core::kit::Set;
use lpd_core::synth::factory::{all, factory, FACTORY_IDS};
use lpd_core::synth::params::*;
use lpd_core::synth::patch::*;
use lpd_core::synth::Synth;

#[test]
fn patches_round_trip_through_json() {
    let p = factory("moogBass").unwrap();
    assert_eq!(Patch::from_json(&p.to_json()).unwrap(), p);
}

#[test]
fn missing_fields_take_defaults_and_names_are_cleaned() {
    let p = Patch::from_json(r#"{"name":"  my lead  "}"#).unwrap();
    assert_eq!((p.name.as_str(), p.category, p.patch_version), ("MY LEAD", Category::Keys, PATCH_VERSION));
    assert_eq!(p.params, SynthParams::default());
    let long = Patch::from_json(r#"{"name":"abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123"}"#).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(long.name, "ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWX");
    assert_eq!(clean_name("   "), "UNTITLED");
}

#[test]
fn values_out_of_range_are_clamped() {
    let p = Patch::from_json(r#"{"params":{"filter":{"cutoff":99999},"oscA":{"unison":12,"pan":-3}}}"#).unwrap();
    assert_eq!((p.params.filter.cutoff, p.params.osc_a.unison, p.params.osc_a.pan), (20_000.0, 7, -1.0));
}

#[test]
fn a_newer_patch_is_refused() {
    let err = Patch::from_json(r#"{"patchVersion":2,"name":"X"}"#).unwrap_err();
    assert!(err.to_string().contains("newer version"), "{err}");
    assert!(Patch::from_json("not json").is_err());
}

#[test]
fn file_names_are_safe_on_windows() {
    assert_eq!(file_name("a/b:c*"), "A_B_C_.lbpatch");
    assert_eq!(file_name("con"), "CON_.lbpatch");
    assert_eq!(file_name("lpt1"), "LPT1_.lbpatch");
    assert_eq!(file_name(""), "UNTITLED.lbpatch");
    assert_eq!(file_name("dots..."), "DOTS.lbpatch");
    assert_eq!(file_name("Moog Bass 2"), "MOOG BASS 2.lbpatch");
}

#[test]
fn every_factory_patch_exists_with_its_name_and_category() {
    let want = [
        ("piano", "PIANO", Category::Piano), ("ePiano", "E.PIANO", Category::Keys), ("organ", "ORGAN", Category::Organ),
        ("synthPad", "SYNTH PAD", Category::Pad), ("synthLead", "SYNTH LEAD", Category::Lead),
        ("synthBass", "SYNTH BASS", Category::Bass), ("moogBass", "MOOG BASS", Category::Bass),
        ("acidBass", "ACID BASS", Category::Bass), ("wobbleBass", "WOBBLE BASS", Category::Bass),
        ("supersaw", "SUPERSAW", Category::Lead), ("syncLead", "SYNC LEAD", Category::Lead),
        ("pluck", "PLUCK", Category::Pluck), ("brass", "BRASS", Category::Lead), ("strings", "STRINGS", Category::Pad),
        ("vowelPad", "VOWEL PAD", Category::Pad), ("fmBell", "FM BELL", Category::Keys),
    ];
    assert_eq!(FACTORY_IDS.len(), 16);
    assert_eq!(all().len(), 16);
    for (id, name, category) in want {
        let p = factory(id).unwrap();
        assert_eq!((p.name.as_str(), p.category), (name, category), "{id}");
    }
    assert!(factory("nope").is_none());
}

#[test]
fn every_factory_patch_keeps_the_wheel_vibrato() {
    for p in all() {
        let ok = p.params.routes.iter().any(|r| {
            matches!(r.source, Source::Lfo1 | Source::Lfo2)
                && r.dest == Some(Dest::OscPitch)
                && (r.amount - 0.021).abs() < 1e-6
                && r.aux == Some(Source::Wheel)
        });
        assert!(ok, "{} has no wheel vibrato", p.name);
    }
}

#[test]
fn every_factory_patch_renders_a_finite_chord_below_full_scale() {
    for p in all() {
        let mut s = Synth::new(48_000);
        s.load_params(&p.params);
        let (mut l, mut r) = (vec![0.0f32; 480], vec![0.0f32; 480]);
        s.render_add(&mut l, &mut r);
        for n in [60, 64, 67] {
            s.note_on(n, 110);
        }
        let (mut l, mut r) = (vec![0.0f32; 48_000], vec![0.0f32; 48_000]);
        for (cl, cr) in l.chunks_mut(512).zip(r.chunks_mut(512)) {
            s.render_add(cl, cr);
        }
        assert!(l.iter().chain(&r).all(|v| v.is_finite()), "{}", p.name);
        let peak = l.iter().chain(&r).fold(0.0f32, |m, v| m.max(v.abs()));
        let rms = (l.iter().map(|v| v * v).sum::<f32>() / l.len() as f32).sqrt();
        assert!(peak < 1.0, "{} peaks at {peak}", p.name);
        assert!(rms > 1e-3, "{} is silent", p.name);
    }
}

#[test]
fn version_1_sets_migrate_to_version_2() {
    let mut v: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_str(&Set::new().to_json()).unwrap();
    v["version"] = serde_json::json!(1);
    v["keys"] = serde_json::json!({ "sound": "organ", "volumeDb": -3.0, "output": 2 });
    let set = Set::from_json(&v.to_string()).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(set.version, 2);
    assert_eq!(set.keys.gm_sound, lpd_core::instrument::KeysSound::Organ);
    assert_eq!(set.keys.patch.name, "ORGAN");
    assert_eq!(set.keys.patch_source, PatchSource::Factory("organ".into()));
    assert_eq!((set.keys.volume_db, set.keys.output, set.keys.modified), (-3.0, Some(2), false));
    v.as_object_mut().unwrap().remove("keys");
    let bare = Set::from_json(&v.to_string()).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(bare.keys.patch.name, "E.PIANO");
    assert_eq!(bare.keys.volume_db, -6.0);
}
```

```rust
use std::path::Path;

#[test]
fn synth_meta_ts_is_up_to_date() {
    let path = Path::new(env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR")).join("../../src/lib/synth-meta.ts");
    let want = lpd_core::synth::export::ui_meta_ts();
    let have = std::fs::read_to_string(&path).unwrap_or_default().replace("\r\n", "\n");
    if have != want {
        std::fs::write(&path, &want).unwrap();
        panic!("src/lib/synth-meta.ts was out of date and has been rewritten — run the tests again and commit it");
    }
}

#[test]
fn the_meta_describes_every_param_and_destination() {
    let meta = lpd_core::synth::export::ui_meta();
    assert_eq!(meta["params"].as_array().unwrap().len(), 109);
    assert_eq!(meta["dests"].as_array().unwrap().len(), 27);
    assert_eq!(meta["sources"][1]["color"], "#ffb21a");
    assert_eq!(meta["lcxl"][1][0], "filter.cutoff");
    assert_eq!(meta["factory"].as_array().unwrap().len(), 16);
    let cutoff = meta["params"].as_array().unwrap().iter().find(|p| p["id"] == "filter.cutoff").unwrap();
    assert_eq!((cutoff["kind"].as_str(), cutoff["taper"].as_str(), cutoff["unit"].as_str()), (Some("float"), Some("log"), Some("hz")));
    assert_eq!(cutoff["dest"], "filter.cutoff");
    assert_eq!(cutoff["default"], 12_000.0);
    let table = meta["params"].as_array().unwrap().iter().find(|p| p["id"] == "oscA.table").unwrap();
    assert_eq!(table["labels"][4], "FM SWEEP");
}
```

`src/lib/synth-types.ts` (complete):

```ts
// Types of the keys synth (keys synth plan, Task 9). They mirror `lpd_core::synth` (serde camelCase).
import type { KeysSound } from './keys.ts';

export type TableId = 'basic' | 'pwm' | 'harmonics' | 'vowels' | 'fmSweep' | 'syncSweep' | 'organ' | 'digital';
export type Warp = 'off' | 'sync' | 'bend' | 'pwm' | 'fm' | 'rm';
export type SubShape = 'sine' | 'triangle' | 'saw' | 'square';
export type NoiseColor = 'white' | 'pink';
export type FilterType = 'lp24' | 'lp12' | 'hp12' | 'bp12' | 'notch';
export type LfoShape = 'sine' | 'triangle' | 'sawUp' | 'sawDown' | 'square' | 'sampleHold' | 'smoothRandom';
export type SyncDiv = '1/32' | '1/16T' | '1/16' | '1/16D' | '1/8T' | '1/8' | '1/8D' | '1/4T' | '1/4' | '1/4D' | '1/2' | '1/1' | '2/1' | '4/1' | '8/1';
export type LfoMode = 'trig' | 'free' | 'oneShot';
export type Polarity = 'bi' | 'uni';
export type VoiceMode = 'poly' | 'mono' | 'legato';
export type GlideMode = 'always' | 'legato';
export type DriveType = 'soft' | 'tube' | 'hard' | 'fold';
export type SourceId = 'env1' | 'env2' | 'env3' | 'lfo1' | 'lfo2' | 'vel' | 'note' | 'rand' | 'wheel' | 'bend';
export type Category = 'piano' | 'keys' | 'organ' | 'pad' | 'lead' | 'bass' | 'pluck' | 'fx';

export interface OscParams {
  on: boolean; table: TableId; pos: number; oct: number; semi: number; fine: number; warp: Warp; warpAmt: number;
  unison: number; detune: number; blend: number; width: number; phaseRand: number; level: number; pan: number; toFilter: boolean;
}
export interface SubParams { on: boolean; shape: SubShape; oct: number; level: number; toFilter: boolean }
export interface NoiseParams { on: boolean; color: NoiseColor; level: number; toFilter: boolean }
export interface FilterParams { on: boolean; type: FilterType; cutoff: number; reso: number; drive: number; keyTrack: number; envAmt: number }
export interface AmpParams { level: number; velSens: number }
export interface EnvParams { attack: number; decay: number; sustain: number; release: number }
export interface LfoParams { shape: LfoShape; rate: number; sync: boolean; syncDiv: SyncDiv; mode: LfoMode; delay: number; phase: number; polarity: Polarity }
export interface VoiceParams { mode: VoiceMode; glide: number; glideMode: GlideMode; bendRange: number; drift: number; volumeDb: number }
export interface DriveFx { on: boolean; type: DriveType; amount: number; tone: number; mix: number }
export interface ChorusFx { on: boolean; rate: number; depth: number; delay: number; feedback: number; mix: number }
export interface DelayFx { on: boolean; time: number; sync: boolean; syncDiv: SyncDiv; feedback: number; pingPong: boolean; tone: number; mix: number }
export interface ReverbFx { on: boolean; size: number; decay: number; damp: number; preDelay: number; mix: number }
export interface FxParams { drive: DriveFx; chorus: ChorusFx; delay: DelayFx; reverb: ReverbFx }
export interface Route { source: SourceId; dest: string | null; amount: number; aux: SourceId | null; bypass: boolean }
export interface SynthParams {
  oscA: OscParams; oscB: OscParams; sub: SubParams; noise: NoiseParams; filter: FilterParams; amp: AmpParams;
  env: EnvParams[]; lfo: LfoParams[]; voice: VoiceParams; routes: Route[]; fx: FxParams;
}
export interface Patch { patchVersion: number; name: string; category: Category; params: SynthParams }
export type PatchSource = { factory: string } | { user: string } | 'init';
export interface PatchRef { id: string; name: string; category: Category }
export interface KeysSettings { patch: Patch; patchSource: PatchSource; modified: boolean; gmSound: KeysSound; volumeDb: number; output: number | null }
export interface KeysMix { gmSound: KeysSound; volumeDb: number; output: number | null }

export type ParamKind = 'bool' | 'choice' | 'int' | 'float';
export type Taper = 'lin' | 'log' | 'cubic';
export type Unit = 'pct' | 'hz' | 'sec' | 'cents' | 'semis' | 'db' | 'pan' | 'deg' | 'envOct' | 'drive24' | 'drive36' | 'oct' | 'voices';
export interface ParamMeta {
  id: string; section: string; label: string; kind: ParamKind; default: number;
  min?: number; max?: number; taper?: Taper; unit?: Unit; choices?: string[]; labels?: string[]; dest?: string;
}
export interface DestMeta { id: string; label: string; param: string | null }
export interface SourceMeta { id: SourceId; label: string; color: string }
export interface CategoryMeta { id: Category; label: string; color: [number, number, number] }
export interface FactoryMeta { id: string; name: string; category: Category; params: SynthParams }
export interface SynthMeta {
  params: ParamMeta[]; dests: DestMeta[]; sources: SourceMeta[]; categories: CategoryMeta[];
  lcxl: string[][]; init: SynthParams; factory: FactoryMeta[];
}
/** The `synth-telemetry` event (plan 0.4). */
export interface SynthTelemetry {
  scope: number[]; spectrum: number[]; curve: number[]; sources: number[]; live: number[];
  env: [number, number][]; lfo: [number, number][]; cutoffHz: number; voices: number; cpu: number; resets: number;
}
```

**Contracts:**

```rust
// audio/engine.rs
pub enum Command {
    // … existing variants …
    /// The pitch wheel −1…1 (K5); each instrument applies its own range.
    KeysPitch(f32),
    KeysSustain(bool),
    KeysTempo(f32),
    SynthParam(ParamId, f32),
    SynthRoute { slot: u8, route: Option<Route> },
    /// A whole patch; the box goes back through the trash.
    SynthLoad(Box<SynthParams>),
}
/// Boxes the audio thread hands back, to be dropped off it.
#[derive(Debug)]
pub enum Trash { Instrument(Box<dyn Instrument>), Params(Box<SynthParams>) }
impl Engine { pub fn take_trash(&mut self) -> Option<Consumer<Trash>>; }   // queue capacity 8

// audio/taps.rs
pub const KEYS_SCOPE_CAPACITY: usize = 8192;
pub const SYNTH_MONITOR_CAPACITY: usize = 64;
pub struct Taps { pub scope: Consumer<f32>, pub levels: Consumer<Levels>, pub keys: Consumer<f32>, pub synth: Consumer<SynthMonitor> }

// instrument/mod.rs — `fn pitch_bend(&mut self, wheel: f32);` doc: "−1…1; the instrument applies its range"
```

**Behaviour:**
- `Engine::build` creates `Synth::new(sample_rate)` as the instrument. `Synth::new` first calls `wavetable::registry()` so the tables are built on the constructing thread, never on the audio thread.
- `handle`: `KeysSustain(d)` → `instrument.sustain(d)`; `KeysTempo(b)` → `instrument.set_tempo(b)`; `SynthParam(id, v)` → `instrument.set_param(id, v)`; `SynthRoute { slot, route }` → `instrument.set_route(slot as usize, route)`; `SynthLoad(b)` → `instrument.load_params(&b)` then the box goes to the trash; `SetInstrument(new)` → the old instrument (after `all_notes_off`) goes to the trash. A full trash queue leaks the box (`std::mem::forget`) instead of freeing it.
- `render_block`: time `instrument.render_add` with `std::time::Instant` (`cpu = elapsed / (n / rate)`); with taps, push `(keys_l[i] + keys_r[i]) / 2` for every frame into `taps.keys` (stop at the first full push) and, when `instrument.monitor()` is `Some(m)`, push `m` with `m.cpu = cpu` into `taps.synth`.
- `SoundFontKeys::pitch_bend(wheel)`: `8192 + wheel·8191` (its RPN range stays ±2 st); `SoundFontKeys::sustain(d)`: CC 64 = 127 or 0.
- Router (`src-tauri`):
  - `trash: Option<Consumer<Trash>>`, `set_trash`, `drain_trash() -> Vec<Trash>`; `AudioSystem.trash` and `AppState::install_trash` take `Consumer<Trash>`.
  - `send_keys` sends `KeysSound(gm_sound)`, `KeysRoute`, then `KeysTempo(set.pattern.bpm)` through the new `send_tempo(&mut self)`.
  - New field `keys_sustain: bool` (false; Task 11 wires CC 64). `send_wheels` sends `KeysPitch(self.keys_pitch)` (normalized), `KeysMod`, `KeysSustain(self.keys_sustain)`; `keys_pitch_value(v)` sends `KeysPitch(v.clamp(-1.0, 1.0))`.
  - New `send_patch(&mut self)`: `SynthLoad(Box::new(self.set.keys.patch.params))`.
  - `Router::new`: mixer, `send_keys`, `send_patch`. `replace_set`: needs `1 + groups + 1 + 3 + 1` free slots, then StopAll, mixer, `send_keys`, `send_patch`. `replace_audio`: mixer, `send_keys`, `send_patch`, `send_wheels`. `set_instrument`: SetInstrument, `send_keys`, `send_patch`, `send_wheels`. `install_set` (undo / redo): `send_keys`, and `send_patch` only when the patch's params differ from the previous set's.
- `AppState`: `KeysStatus::builtin()` becomes `KeysStatus::synth()` = `{ engine: "synth", name: "SYNTH", error: null }` (K7); `load_keys_instrument(None)` installs `Box::new(Synth::new(rate))`.
- `src-tauri/src/lib.rs` `run()`: before the audio controller starts, `std::thread::spawn(|| { lpd_core::synth::wavetable::registry(); });` (the first stream start does not wait for the tables).

```rust
#[test]
fn synth_commands_reach_the_synth_and_its_monitor() {
    let (mut engine, _events, mut taps) = Engine::with_taps(SR);
    engine.handle(Command::SynthParam(lpd_core::synth::params::ParamId::FilterCutoff, 300.0));
    engine.handle(Command::KeysNoteOn { note: 60, velocity: 100 });
    for _ in 0..12 {
        render(&mut engine, 512);
    }
    let mut last = None;
    while let Ok(m) = taps.synth.pop() {
        last = Some(m);
    }
    let m = last.expect("a monitor per block");
    assert!((m.cutoff_hz / 300.0 - 1.0).abs() < 0.05, "{}", m.cutoff_hz);
    assert_eq!(m.voices, 1);
    assert!(m.cpu > 0.0);
    let mut any = false;
    while let Ok(s) = taps.keys.pop() {
        any |= s.abs() > 1e-3;
    }
    assert!(any, "the keys scope carries the synth");
}

#[test]
fn a_synth_load_returns_its_box() {
    let (mut engine, _) = Engine::new(SR);
    let mut trash = engine.take_trash().unwrap();
    engine.handle(Command::SynthLoad(Box::new(lpd_core::synth::params::SynthParams::default())));
    assert!(matches!(trash.pop(), Ok(Trash::Params(_))));
}

#[test]
fn keys_pitch_is_the_normalized_wheel() {
    use lpd_core::synth::params::SynthParams;
    let crossings = |wheel: f32| {
        let (mut engine, _) = Engine::new(SR);
        let mut p = SynthParams::default();
        p.osc_a.pos = 0.0; // sine
        p.filter.on = false;
        engine.handle(Command::SynthLoad(Box::new(p)));
        render(&mut engine, 960);
        engine.handle(Command::KeysPitch(wheel));
        engine.handle(Command::KeysNoteOn { note: 60, velocity: 100 });
        render(&mut engine, 4800);
        let x = left(&render(&mut engine, 48_000));
        x.windows(2).filter(|w| w[0] < 0.0 && w[1] >= 0.0).count() as f32
    };
    let ratio = crossings(1.0) / crossings(0.0);
    assert!((ratio - 2f32.powf(2.0 / 12.0)).abs() < 0.01, "{ratio}");
}
```

**Contracts:**

```rust
// patches.rs — the user library (spec 8.4). No locks are held while these run.
#[derive(Serialize, Clone, Debug, PartialEq)] #[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub struct PatchRef { pub id: String, pub name: String, pub category: Category }
#[derive(Serialize, Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Default)] #[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub struct PatchList { pub factory: Vec<PatchRef>, pub user: Vec<PatchRef> }
pub fn factory_list() -> Vec<PatchRef>;                                  // FACTORY_IDS order, id = factory id
pub fn list(dir: &Path) -> Vec<PatchRef>;                                 // *.lbpatch that parse, by name; id = file name
pub fn read(dir: &Path, file: &str) -> Result<Patch, String>;             // "Patch file not found" / the parse error
pub fn write(dir: &Path, patch: &Patch, overwrite: bool) -> Result<String, String>; // creates dir; tmp + rename; Err("exists")
pub fn rename(dir: &Path, file: &str, name: &str) -> Result<PatchRef, String>;      // Err("exists") if another file has the new name
pub fn delete(dir: &Path, file: &str) -> Result<(), String>;
// Every `file` argument containing '/', '\\' or ".." is refused with "Not a patch file name".

// router.rs
pub fn send_synth_param(&mut self, id: ParamId, value: f32);   // Command::SynthParam
pub fn send_route(&mut self, slot: usize, route: Option<Route>); // Command::SynthRoute
pub fn keys_sustain(&mut self, down: bool);                     // remembers it, Command::KeysSustain
// (`send_tempo` and `send_patch` exist since Task 10.)

// state.rs
#[derive(Deserialize, Debug, Clone, PartialEq)] #[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub enum PatchRequest { Factory(String), User(String), Init(bool) }   // JS { factory } | { user } | { init: true }
impl AppState {
    pub fn synth_param(&self, id: &str, value: f32) -> Result<(), String>;
    pub fn synth_route(&self, slot: usize, route: Option<Route>) -> Result<(), String>;
    pub fn synth_patches(&self, dir: &Path) -> PatchList;
    pub fn load_patch(&self, request: PatchRequest, dir: &Path) -> Result<Set, String>;
    pub fn save_patch(&self, name: &str, overwrite: bool, dir: &Path) -> Result<PatchRef, String>;
    pub fn rename_patch(&self, file: &str, name: &str, dir: &Path) -> Result<PatchRef, String>;
    pub fn delete_patch(&self, file: &str, dir: &Path) -> Result<(), String>;
    pub fn revert_patch(&self, dir: &Path) -> Result<Set, String>;
    pub fn set_keys_engine(&self, engine: &str) -> Result<KeysStatus, String>;
}

// config.rs
pub keys_engine: Option<String>,             // in AppConfig (camelCase "keysEngine")
impl AppConfig { pub fn engine(&self) -> &'static str; }  // "soundfont" when keys_engine is Some("soundfont") and soundfont is set, or keys_engine is None and soundfont is set; else "synth"

// commands.rs (registered in lib.rs)
fn patches_dir(app: &AppHandle) -> Result<PathBuf, String>;   // <Documents>\Launchpad Drummer\Patches
#[tauri::command] fn synth_param(id: String, value: f32, state) -> Result<(), String>;
#[tauri::command] fn synth_route(slot: usize, route: Option<Route>, state) -> Result<(), String>;
#[tauri::command] async fn synth_patches(app, state) -> Result<PatchList, String>;
#[tauri::command] async fn synth_load_patch(source: PatchRequest, app, state) -> Result<Set, String>;
#[tauri::command] async fn synth_save_patch(name: String, overwrite: bool, app, state) -> Result<PatchRef, String>;
#[tauri::command] async fn synth_rename_patch(file: String, name: String, app, state) -> Result<PatchRef, String>;
#[tauri::command] async fn synth_delete_patch(file: String, app, state) -> Result<(), String>;
#[tauri::command] async fn synth_revert(app, state) -> Result<Set, String>;
#[tauri::command] fn synth_table(table: TableId) -> TableFrames;          // { frames: table(t).display(128) }
#[tauri::command] async fn set_keys_engine(engine: String, app, state) -> Result<KeysStatus, String>; // emits keys-status
#[tauri::command] async fn patches_folder(app) -> Result<String, String>; // the path (created)
```

**Behaviour (exact):**
- **`synth_param`:** unknown id → `Err("Unknown synth control: {id}")`. Under one router lock: `edit_in` with key `synth.{id}` sets the value through `ParamId::set` and sets `keys.modified = true` when the value changed; then `send_synth_param(id, stored value)`; `touch()` after the lock.
- **`synth_route`:** `slot ≥ 16`, or appending to 16 routes → `Err("Matrix full (16 routes)")`; a slot past the end → `Err("No route in slot {slot + 1}")`. A replace uses the undo key `synth.route.{slot}` (a depth drag is one step); an add or a delete has no key (one step each). Success sets `modified`, sends `SynthRoute`, `touch()`.
- **`load_patch`:** reads the file first (no lock; `Err("Patch not loaded — {reason}")` on failure; unknown factory id → `Err("Unknown factory patch: {id}")`), then one `edit_in` (no key) that sets `patch`, `patchSource` and `modified = false`, then `send_patch()`. Returns the set.
- **`save_patch`:** copies the current patch, sets its name to `clean_name(name)`, `patches::write` (→ `Err("exists")` unless `overwrite`), then — without an undo step (0.2 item 2) — sets the set's patch name, `patchSource = User(file)` and `modified = false`; `touch()`.
- **`rename_patch` / `delete_patch`:** file operations; when the current patch came from that file, rename updates its name and source, delete turns the source into `Init` and keeps the sound (no undo step).
- **`revert_patch`:** reloads the source like `load_patch` (a missing user file → `Err("Patch file not found")`, nothing changes).
- **Engine switch (K6, K7):** `set_keys_engine("synth")` saves `keysEngine: "synth"` and installs a fresh `Synth` (the router then sends keys, patch and wheels); `set_keys_engine("soundfont")` needs `config.soundfont` (`Err("Load a SoundFont first")`), saves `keysEngine: "soundfont"` and loads it as today. `set_soundfont(Some(p))` also sets `keysEngine: "soundfont"`; `set_soundfont(None)` sets `keysEngine: "synth"`. After an audio restart, `reload_soundfont` reloads the `.sf2` only when `config.engine() == "soundfont"`; otherwise the status is `KeysStatus::synth()`.
- **Sustain pedal:** `keys_event` handles `Cc { cc: 64, value }` → `keys_sustain(value >= 64)` (no UI event). `reset_keys_wheels` also lifts the pedal (sends `KeysSustain(false)` when it was down).
- **Tempo (K4):** `seq_update` and a learned `seq.bpm` call `send_tempo()` when `pattern.bpm` changed (same router lock as the edit).

### Task 12: LCXL knob map and LEDs, learned synth CCs, `synth-params`, `keys-notice` and `synth-telemetry`

**Purpose.** Spec 10.2 (LCXL), 10.3 (learn), 2.4 and 2.6 (events), K18, K20.

**Files:**
- Modify: `crates/lpd-core/src/kit/learn.rs`, `crates/lpd-core/src/audio/fft.rs` (`log_freqs`, `log_bands`), `crates/lpd-core/tests/kit_learn.rs`, `crates/lpd-core/tests/audio_fft.rs`, `src-tauri/src/router.rs`, `src-tauri/src/state.rs`, `src-tauri/src/lcxl.rs`, `src-tauri/src/telemetry.rs`, `src-tauri/tests/telemetry.rs`

**Contracts:**

```rust
// learn.rs — synth ids are "synth." + a ParamId id
pub fn synth_param(id: &str) -> Option<crate::synth::params::ParamId>;
// parse_id: `synth.<id>` → ParamId::Synth(p); param_value: Bool kinds → Toggle, others → Continuous(kind.from_norm(cc / 127));
// apply_set_param: Continuous → p.set(&mut set.keys.patch.params, v); Toggle → flip; either sets keys.modified when it changed.

// fft.rs
pub fn log_freqs(n: usize, lo: f32, hi: f32) -> Vec<f32>;                    // lo·(hi/lo)^(i/(n−1))
pub fn log_bands(mags: &[f32; FFT_SIZE / 2], sample_rate: u32, freqs: &[f32]) -> Vec<f32>;
// dBFS at each frequency: the loudest bin between the geometric midpoints to its neighbours,
// the nearest bin when that span holds none; floor −100.

// router.rs
pub struct LcxlOutcome { pub mixer: bool, pub synth: Option<(ParamId, f32)> }
pub fn on_lcxl(&mut self, ev: LcxlEvent, synth_engine: bool) -> LcxlOutcome;
// Knob { row, ch, value } with synth_engine → synth: Some((SYNTH_KNOBS[row][ch], kind.from_norm(value / 127)));
// without → nothing. Faders and buttons unchanged (mixer).

// state.rs
pub fn take_synth_changes(&self) -> Vec<(String, f32)>;   // drained by the LCXL thread every 33 ms
pub fn take_keys_notice(&self) -> Option<String>;

// telemetry.rs
#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, PartialEq)] #[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub struct SynthTelemetry {
    pub scope: Vec<f32>, pub spectrum: Vec<f32>, pub curve: Vec<f32>, pub sources: Vec<f32>, pub live: Vec<f32>,
    pub env: Vec<[f32; 2]>, pub lfo: Vec<[f32; 2]>, pub cutoff_hz: f32, pub voices: u8, pub cpu: f32, pub resets: u32,
}
pub struct Tick { pub frame: Option<TelemetryFrame>, pub synth: Option<SynthTelemetry>, pub dropouts_changed: bool, pub lost: Option<AudioStatus> }
impl Telemetry { pub fn tick_at(&mut self, state: &AppState, now: Instant) -> Tick; }   // `tick` = `tick_at(Instant::now())`
```

**Behaviour (exact):**
- **LCXL knobs** (`AppState::on_lcxl`): `synth_engine = keys_status().engine == "synth"`, read before taking the router lock. A synth knob move is an undoable edit with key `synth.{id}` (coalesced), sets `modified`, sends `SynthParam`, and records `(id, value)` for `synth-params`. A knob move while the SoundFont plays changes nothing and queues the notice "LCXL knobs drive the synth — SoundFont active" once per engine change. The learn-armed message stays "LCXL controls are fixed in Part 1".
- **Learned synth CCs** (`on_midi`): the router puts `synth.*` ids in `out.set_param` like `seq.bpm`; `AppState` applies them with key `learn:{id}`, then sends `SynthParam` with the stored value and records the change for `synth-params`. A learned `seq.bpm` sends `KeysTempo`.
- **`synth-params`:** the LCXL thread (its loop runs with or without a device) emits `{ values: [[id, value], …] }` at most every 33 ms (last value per id, the newest always follows), and `keys-notice` `{ text }` when one is queued.
- **Knob LEDs:** `LedState` gains the synth flag; `led_updates` sends the 24 knob LEDs (`lcxl::knob_led_index`, template 0) whenever the flag differs from what the device last got (or on first contact): row 0 `Amber`, row 1 `Red`, row 2 `Green` while the synth engine plays, `Off` otherwise.
- **Telemetry:** the thread keeps a second 2048-sample ring for `taps.keys` and the newest `SynthMonitor` from `taps.synth` with its arrival time. When the feed is on and a monitor arrived within the last 250 ms, the tick builds `SynthTelemetry`: `scope` = 256 samples starting at `trigger_index` of the ordered ring; `spectrum` = `log_bands(fft(keys ring), rate, &freqs)` with `freqs = log_freqs(128, 20.0, 20_000.0)` (computed once); `curve` = `filter::response_db(monitor.filter, monitor.cutoff_hz, monitor.reso, monitor.drive, &freqs)`; the other fields copied from the monitor (`env`, `lfo` as `[f32; 2]` pairs). `run()` emits it as `synth-telemetry`.

  - `audio_fft.rs`: `log_freqs_span_the_range` (`log_freqs(128, 20.0, 20_000.0)` starts at 20 and ends at 20 000 within 0.01, strictly increasing); `log_bands_find_a_sine` (a full-scale sine on bin 100 → the band whose centre is nearest 100 × 23.4375 Hz reads above −1 dBFS, a band two octaves away below −60).
  - `src-tauri/src/router.rs` tests: `lcxl_knobs_drive_the_synth_only_with_the_synth_engine` (row 1 ch 0 value 127 → `Some((FilterCutoff, 20000.0))`; with `false` → `None` and the mixer unchanged); `lcxl_faders_still_drive_the_mixer`.
  - `src-tauri/src/state.rs` tests: `an_lcxl_synth_turn_is_undoable_and_reported` (turn → `take_synth_changes()` = `[("filter.cutoff", 20000.0)]`, undo restores); `a_knob_with_the_soundfont_queues_one_notice`.
  - `src-tauri/src/lcxl.rs` tests: `knob_leds_follow_the_engine` (first update sends 24 knob messages amber/red/green; switching the flag sends 24 `Off`s; no change sends none).

### Task 13: Layout solver, slim mixer, channel popover, synth plate shell

**Purpose.** Spec 6.1 (size rules, K16, K17), 6.2 (slim mixer and channel popover, K13), the synth plate frame of 6.3.

**Files:**
- Create: `src/components/MixerSlim.svelte`, `src/components/ChannelPopover.svelte`, `src/components/SynthPlate.svelte`
- Modify: `src/lib/layout.ts` (rewrite), `src/lib/layout.test.ts` (rewrite), `src/App.svelte`
- Delete: `src/components/MixerStrip.svelte`

**`src/lib/layout.ts`** (complete):

```ts
// Play screen size solver (UI spec 4, keys synth spec 6.1). Pure: bay size in, sizes out.
// The window height alone sets the Launchpad row, the keybed and the sequencer. Across the row the
// Launchpad, the slim mixer and the synth share the width; the synth keeps at least 480 px.

export interface PlayLayout {
  /** Pad size actually drawn. */
  pad: number;
  /** Pad size the height allows: it sets the row, the keybed and the sequencer. */
  pH: number;
  /** Height of the Launchpad row = height of the mixer and synth plates. */
  rowH: number;
  docked: boolean;
  /** Telemetry column width; -10 = hidden, 30 = folded strip. */
  tw: number;
  mini: boolean;
  kh: number; // keybed height
  sq: number; // sequencer plate height
  lh: number; // sequencer lane height
  mixMode: 'slim' | 'strip';
  mixW: number;
  synthW: number;
}

export interface PlayLayoutInput {
  width: number;
  height: number;
  drawerOpen: boolean;
  drawerPinned: boolean;
  teleFolded: boolean;
  /** Keys plate and sequencer folded to a 28 px strip; the height they save goes to the row. */
  keysFolded?: boolean;
  seqFolded?: boolean;
  lanes: number;
}

export type SynthPageSet = 'wide' | 'narrow' | 'compact';

/** A folded keys plate or sequencer. */
export const FOLDED_H = 28;
const GAP = 5;
const DRAWER_W = 300;
const DTAB_W = 28;
export const TELE_MINI = 30;
export const LP_CHROME_H = 16 + 22 + 2 * 6 + 37; // Launchpad plate minus the 8 pad rows
export const LP_CHROME_W = 24 + 28 + 66 + 12 + 7 * GAP; // Launchpad plate minus the 8 pad columns
const PLATE_GAP = 10;
export const MIX_SLIM_W = 300;
export const MIX_STRIP_W = 40;
export const SYNTH_MIN = 480;
export const SYNTH_ONE_PAGE = 760;
const PAD_KEEP = 56;
const PAD_MIN = 36;
const PAD_MAX = 112;

const clamp = (v: number, lo: number, hi: number) => Math.min(hi, Math.max(lo, v));

export function solvePlayLayout(input: PlayLayoutInput): PlayLayout {
  const { width: W, height: H } = input;
  const keysOpen = !input.keysFolded;
  const seqOpen = !input.seqFolded;
  const avail = H - 16 - LP_CHROME_H - 7 * GAP - 2 * 8;
  const sq0 = seqOpen ? clamp(avail * 0.25, 110, 320) : FOLDED_H;
  const rest = avail - sq0;
  const raw = keysOpen ? Math.min((rest - 12 - 44) / 8, (rest - 12) / 8.9) : (rest - FOLDED_H) / 8;
  const pH = Math.floor(clamp(raw, PAD_MIN, PAD_MAX));
  const kh = keysOpen ? clamp(Math.round(pH * 0.9), 44, 110) : FOLDED_H;
  const sq = seqOpen ? clamp(Math.floor(avail - 8 * pH - (keysOpen ? 12 + kh : FOLDED_H)), 110, 360) : FOLDED_H;
  const lanes = Math.max(1, input.lanes);
  const lh = clamp(Math.floor((sq - 12 - 22 - 6 + 3) / lanes) - 3, 14, 24);

  const fit = (docked: boolean, tw: number, mix: 'slim' | 'strip') => {
    const stageW = W - 16 - DTAB_W - 10 - (docked ? DRAWER_W + 10 : 0) - (tw + 10);
    const mixW = mix === 'slim' ? MIX_SLIM_W : MIX_STRIP_W;
    const room = stageW - LP_CHROME_W - 2 * PLATE_GAP - mixW;
    // Rule 3: pads give way until the synth has one page, but not below 56 px. Rule 2: the synth keeps 480 px.
    let p = Math.min(pH, Math.max(Math.floor((room - SYNTH_ONE_PAGE) / 8), Math.min(PAD_KEEP, pH)));
    p = Math.min(p, Math.floor((room - SYNTH_MIN) / 8));
    return { p, room, mixW, ok: p >= PAD_MIN };
  };
  // Telemetry: the widest of 300…230 px that costs the pads and the synth nothing, else 220.
  const pickTele = (dock: boolean) => {
    if (input.teleFolded) return TELE_MINI;
    const bare = fit(dock, -10, 'slim');
    for (let w = 300; w > 220; w -= 10) {
      const f = fit(dock, w, 'slim');
      if (f.ok && f.p >= bare.p && f.room - 8 * f.p >= Math.min(bare.room - 8 * bare.p, SYNTH_ONE_PAGE)) return w;
    }
    return 220;
  };
  // Rule 2's order: pads (inside fit), then a docked drawer floats, the telemetry folds, the mixer collapses.
  let docked = input.drawerOpen && input.drawerPinned;
  let mix: 'slim' | 'strip' = 'slim';
  let tw = pickTele(docked);
  if (!fit(docked, tw, mix).ok && docked) {
    docked = false;
    tw = pickTele(false);
  }
  if (!fit(docked, tw, mix).ok) tw = TELE_MINI;
  if (!fit(docked, tw, mix).ok) mix = 'strip';
  const f = fit(docked, tw, mix);
  const pad = Math.max(PAD_MIN, f.p);
  return {
    pad, pH, rowH: LP_CHROME_H + 7 * GAP + 8 * pH, docked, tw, mini: tw > 0 && tw <= TELE_MINI,
    kh, sq, lh, mixMode: mix, mixW: f.mixW, synthW: f.room - 8 * pad,
  };
}

/** The synth plate's page set (spec 6.3, K8). */
export function synthPageSet(width: number, height: number): SynthPageSet {
  if (height < 480) return 'compact';
  return width >= SYNTH_ONE_PAGE && height >= 600 ? 'wide' : 'narrow';
}

export const SYNTH_PAGES: Record<SynthPageSet, readonly string[]> = {
  wide: ['MAIN', 'FX', 'MATRIX'],
  narrow: ['OSC', 'MOD', 'FX', 'MATRIX'],
  compact: ['OSC', 'FILTER', 'ENV', 'LFO', 'FX', 'MATRIX'],
};

/** Knob size on the synth plate: 17 % of a row of sections, 24–44 px. */
export function synthKnob(height: number, set: SynthPageSet): number {
  const rows = set === 'wide' ? 3 : set === 'narrow' ? 2 : 1;
  return clamp(Math.round(((height - 64) / rows) * 0.17), 24, 44);
}
```

**`src/lib/layout.test.ts`** (complete rewrite; `base = { drawerOpen: true, drawerPinned: true, teleFolded: false, lanes: 5 }`, bays: 2560×1440 → 2455×1296, 1920×1080 → 1816×988, 1280×720 → 1176×628):
- `2560x1440 with the drawer floating keeps 94 px pads and gives the synth one page` → `{ pad: 94, tw: 300, synthW: 854, mixMode: 'slim', docked: false }`.
- `2560x1440 with the drawer docked` → `{ docked: true, tw: 220, pad: 77, synthW: 760 }`.
- `1920x1080: a docked drawer floats so the synth keeps its room` → `{ docked: false, pad: 56, tw: 220, synthW: 599 }`.
- `1280x720: telemetry and mixer collapse` → `{ tw: 30, mini: true, mixMode: 'strip', pad: 37, synthW: 561 }`.
- `the keybed and sequencer follow the height alone` → at 2455×1296 docked and floating: same `kh` 85, `sq` 293, `rowH` 874; at 1176×628: `kh` 44, `sq` 122.
- `folding the sequencer gives its height to the row` → 1176×628 seq folded: `pH` 48, `rowH` 506, `sq` 28, `pad` 47.
- `folding both plates` → `pH` 52, `kh` 28, `sq` 28.
- `pads never exceed 112 px` → 5000×3000, open and both folded: `pad` 112.
- `above the minimum window the synth never drops under 480 px` → for every width 1176…2600 (step 50) at heights 628 and 988, plates open, drawer floating and docked: `synthW >= 480`.
- `below the minimum window the synth may be narrower (K17)` → 900×628: `synthW` 293.
- `page sets and knob sizes` → `synthPageSet(854, 874)` 'wide'; `(760, 874)` 'wide'; `(599, 666)` 'narrow'; `(561, 418)` 'compact'; `synthKnob(874, 'wide')` 44; `synthKnob(418, 'compact')` 44; `synthKnob(300, 'wide')` 24 (13 px, clamped to the minimum).

**MixerSlim.svelte** (replaces `MixerStrip` on PLAY):

```ts
let { groups, masterDb, height, mode, inputs = [], onupdategroup, onupdatemaster, onchannel, onexpand }: {
  groups: Group[]; masterDb: number; height: number; mode: 'slim' | 'strip';
  /** Per channel: pads routed to it, the keys' colour and label when they play through it. */
  inputs?: { pads: number; keysColor: string | null; keysLabel: string }[];
  onupdategroup: (index: number, group: Group) => void;
  onupdatemaster: (masterDb: number) => void;
  /** A click on a channel chip (double-click: `rename` true). */
  onchannel: (index: number, x: number, y: number, rename: boolean) => void;
  /** Strip mode: open the slim mixer as a popover. */
  onexpand?: (x: number, y: number) => void;
} = $props();
```

- `mode === 'slim'`: `<div class="mixer plate slim" aria-label="Mixer" data-label="MIX" style:height="{height}px">`, 8 × `.ms-col[data-group=i]` + `.ms-col.ms-master`. A column top to bottom: `<button class="ms-chip" style:--c={groupColor}>` (text `i + 1`; a `.kdot` in `inputs[i].keysColor` before it when set; `title` = `{groupLegend(i, name)} — click for pan, pitch, low-pass and inputs`; `aria-haspopup="dialog"`; click → `onchannel(i, rect.left, rect.bottom + 2, false)`, dblclick → `onchannel(…, true)`), `<Fader label="{name} volume" value={volumeDb} height={faderH} meters={[i]} learnId="group.{i}.volume" …/>`, `<span class="lcd ms-db">{fmtDbShort(volumeDb)}</span>`, M and S keys (`.key.ms-key`, `class:on` + `amber` / `green`, `aria-pressed`, `aria-label="{name} mute"` / `"{name} solo"`, `data-learn` + `use:learnable` with `group.{i}.mute` / `group.{i}.solo`, exactly as `MixerStrip` did). MASTER column: a `.silk` "MST" label instead of the chip, `<Fader label="Master volume" … color="#ff5b1f" meters={['L', 'R']} learnId="master">`, its dB LCD. `faderH = height − 24 − 22 − 16 − 2·20 − 4·4` (padding, chip, LCD, two keys, gaps).
- `mode === 'strip'`: `<button class="mixer plate strip" aria-label="Mixer — click to open" data-label="MIX" style:height="{height}px">` with 8 `<i class="ms-dot" data-chip={i} style:--c={groupColor}>` (the activity loop drives `[data-chip]` opacity) and a master meter `<span class="ms-meter"><i class="f-fill" data-ch="L"></i><i class="f-fill" data-ch="R"></i></span>`; click → `onexpand(rect.left, rect.top)`.
- CSS (scoped): `.mixer.slim { flex: none; width: 300px; box-sizing: border-box; display: flex; gap: 2px; padding: 12px 8px; }` `.ms-col { width: 30px; display: flex; flex-direction: column; align-items: center; gap: 4px; --fw: 22px; }` `.ms-master { width: 36px; margin-left: 4px; padding-left: 4px; border-left: 1px solid var(--line); --fw: 26px; }` `.ms-chip { width: 26px; height: 22px; border: 0; border-radius: 3px; background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--c) 28%, var(--glass)); box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 1px var(--c); color: var(--lcd); font: 400 11px/22px var(--f-lcd); cursor: pointer; display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center; gap: 2px; }` `.ms-key { width: 26px; height: 20px; min-width: 0; padding: 0; font-size: 10px; }` `.ms-db { font-size: 9.5px; }` `.mixer.strip { flex: none; width: 40px; padding: 12px 4px; display: flex; flex-direction: column; align-items: center; gap: 6px; cursor: pointer; border: 0; color: inherit; }` `.ms-dot { width: 10px; height: 10px; border-radius: 50%; background: var(--c); opacity: .16; }` `.ms-meter { position: relative; flex: 1; width: 12px; }` `.ms-meter .f-fill { position: absolute; top: 0; bottom: 0; width: 4px; background: linear-gradient(0deg, var(--ok) 0 72%, var(--warn) 72% 90%, var(--error) 90%); clip-path: inset(calc(100% - var(--m, 0) * 100%) 0 0 0); } .ms-meter .f-fill[data-ch="R"] { left: 6px; }`.

**ChannelPopover.svelte:**

```ts
let { index, group, inputs, keysHere, keysWhere, x, y, rename = false, onupdategroup, onkeys, onclose }: {
  index: number; group: Group; inputs: { pads: number; keysColor: string | null; keysLabel: string };
  /** The keys play through this channel; `keysWhere` = the row text ("Keys: E.PIANO" or "Keys: on 3 TOMS"). */
  keysHere: boolean; keysWhere: string; x: number; y: number;
  /** Open with the name field ready to type. */
  rename?: boolean;
  onupdategroup: (index: number, group: Group) => void;
  onkeys: () => void;
  onclose: () => void;
} = $props();
```

### Task 14: Synth model helpers and controls

**Purpose.** Spec 4.5 (arcs, live dot, XL tag), 6.3 (accessible controls), K1: the TypeScript side of the parameter model and the four controls the sections use.

**Files:**
- Create: `src/lib/synth.ts`, `src/lib/synth.test.ts`, `src/components/ParamKnob.svelte`, `src/components/ParamFader.svelte`, `src/components/ParamStep.svelte`, `src/components/ChoiceLcd.svelte`, `src/components/ParamKey.svelte`
- Modify: `src/components/Knob.svelte` (optional mod props), `src/lib/learnable.ts` (`extra` menu items)

**`src/lib/synth.ts`** (complete):

```ts
// Keys synth helpers (keys synth plan, Task 14). Pure; the metadata comes from synth-meta.ts.
import { SYNTH_META } from './synth-meta.ts';
import { fmtPan } from './mix.ts';
import type { Category, ParamMeta, SourceId, SynthParams } from './synth-types.ts';

export const PARAMS: ReadonlyMap<string, ParamMeta> = new Map(SYNTH_META.params.map((p) => [p.id, p]));

export function meta(id: string): ParamMeta {
  const m = PARAMS.get(id);
  if (!m) throw new Error(`unknown synth control ${id}`);
  return m;
}

/** The object and field a dotted id points at: `env1.attack` → (p.env[0], 'attack'), `fx.reverb.mix` → (p.fx.reverb, 'mix'). */
function slot(p: SynthParams, id: string): [Record<string, unknown>, string] {
  const parts = id.split('.');
  const key = parts.pop()!;
  let obj: unknown = p;
  for (const part of parts) {
    const m = /^(env|lfo)(\d)$/.exec(part);
    const o = obj as Record<string, unknown>;
    obj = m ? (o[m[1]] as unknown[])[Number(m[2]) - 1] : o[part];
  }
  return [obj as Record<string, unknown>, key];
}

/** Like Rust `Kind::clamp` (NaN → the minimum). */
export function clampValue(m: ParamMeta, v: number): number {
  const x = Number.isNaN(v) ? -Infinity : v;
  switch (m.kind) {
    case 'bool': return x >= 0.5 ? 1 : 0;
    case 'choice': return Math.min(m.choices!.length - 1, Math.max(0, Math.round(x)));
    case 'int': return Math.min(m.max!, Math.max(m.min!, Math.round(x)));
    default: return Math.min(m.max!, Math.max(m.min!, x));
  }
}

/** Numeric value (bools 0 / 1, selectors by index), like Rust `ParamId::get`. */
export function getParam(p: SynthParams, id: string): number {
  const m = meta(id);
  const [obj, key] = slot(p, id);
  const v = obj[key];
  if (m.kind === 'bool') return v ? 1 : 0;
  if (m.kind === 'choice') return Math.max(0, m.choices!.indexOf(String(v)));
  return Number(v);
}

/** Writes a value (clamped) into `p` and returns the number stored. */
export function setParam(p: SynthParams, id: string, value: number): number {
  const m = meta(id);
  const v = clampValue(m, value);
  const [obj, key] = slot(p, id);
  obj[key] = m.kind === 'bool' ? v >= 0.5 : m.kind === 'choice' ? m.choices![v] : v;
  return v;
}

/** Value → knob position 0–1 (Rust `Kind::to_norm`). */
export function toNorm(m: ParamMeta, v: number): number {
  let n: number;
  if (m.kind === 'bool') n = v >= 0.5 ? 1 : 0;
  else if (m.kind === 'choice') n = m.choices!.length > 1 ? v / (m.choices!.length - 1) : 0;
  else if (m.kind === 'int') n = (v - m.min!) / Math.max(1, m.max! - m.min!);
  else if (m.taper === 'log') n = Math.log(Math.max(v, m.min!) / m.min!) / Math.log(m.max! / m.min!);
  else if (m.taper === 'cubic') n = Math.cbrt(Math.max(0, v) / m.max!);
  else n = (v - m.min!) / (m.max! - m.min!);
  return Number.isNaN(n) ? 0 : Math.min(1, Math.max(0, n));
}

/** Knob position 0–1 → value (Rust `Kind::from_norm`). */
export function fromNorm(m: ParamMeta, n: number): number {
  const t = Number.isNaN(n) ? 0 : Math.min(1, Math.max(0, n));
  if (m.kind === 'bool') return t >= 0.5 ? 1 : 0;
  if (m.kind === 'choice') return Math.round(t * (m.choices!.length - 1));
  if (m.kind === 'int') return Math.round(m.min! + t * (m.max! - m.min!));
  if (m.taper === 'log') return m.min! * Math.pow(m.max! / m.min!, t);
  if (m.taper === 'cubic') return m.max! * t * t * t;
  return m.min! + t * (m.max! - m.min!);
}

function signed(v: number, unit: string, digits = 0): string {
  const s = Math.abs(v).toFixed(digits);
  if (Number(s) === 0) return `${(0).toFixed(digits)}${unit}`;
  return `${v > 0 ? '+' : '−'}${s}${unit}`;
}

/** A value in its units: `12.0 kHz`, `632 Hz`, `0.71 Hz`, `2.0 ms`, `1.25 s`, `−7 ct`, `+3.6 oct`, `L20`, `90°`. */
export function fmtParam(id: string, v: number): string {
  const m = meta(id);
  if (m.kind === 'bool') return v >= 0.5 ? 'ON' : 'OFF';
  if (m.kind === 'choice') return m.labels![Math.round(v)] ?? '';
  switch (m.unit) {
    case 'pct': return `${Math.round(v * 100)} %`;
    case 'hz': return v >= 1000 ? `${(v / 1000).toFixed(v >= 10_000 ? 1 : 2)} kHz` : `${v.toFixed(v < 10 ? 2 : v < 100 ? 1 : 0)} Hz`;
    case 'sec': return v < 1 ? `${(v * 1000).toFixed(v < 0.01 ? 1 : 0)} ms` : `${v.toFixed(2)} s`;
    case 'cents': return signed(Math.round(v), ' ct');
    case 'semis': return signed(Math.round(v), ' st');
    case 'db': return v <= -60 ? '−∞' : `${signed(v, '', 1)} dB`;
    case 'pan': return fmtPan(v);
    case 'deg': return `${Math.round(v * 360)}°`;
    case 'envOct': return `${signed(v * 8, '', 1)} oct`;
    case 'drive24': return `${(v * 24).toFixed(1)} dB`;
    case 'drive36': return `${(v * 36).toFixed(1)} dB`;
    case 'oct': return signed(Math.round(v), '');
    case 'voices': return `${Math.round(v)}`;
    default: return String(v);
  }
}

/** A route amount in its destination's units: pitch ±24 st, cutoff in octaves (≈ 10 per 100 %), else %. */
export function fmtAmount(dest: string | null, amount: number): string {
  if (dest && dest.endsWith('pitch')) return signed(amount * 24, ' st', 2);
  if (dest === 'filter.cutoff') return signed(amount * Math.log2(1000), ' oct', 1);
  return signed(Math.round(amount * 100), ' %');
}

export const sourceColor = (id: SourceId) => SYNTH_META.sources.find((s) => s.id === id)?.color ?? '#c9d1d9';
export const sourceLabel = (id: SourceId) => SYNTH_META.sources.find((s) => s.id === id)?.label ?? id;
export const destLabel = (id: string) => SYNTH_META.dests.find((d) => d.id === id)?.label ?? id;
export const categoryColor = (c: Category): [number, number, number] =>
  SYNTH_META.categories.find((x) => x.id === c)?.color ?? [63, 40, 0];

/** The destination a control is, if any. */
export const destOf = (id: string): string | undefined => PARAMS.get(id)?.dest;

/** "LCXL row 2 · knob 1" for the 24 mapped controls, else null. */
export function lcxlTag(id: string): string | null {
  for (let row = 0; row < SYNTH_META.lcxl.length; row++) {
    const ch = SYNTH_META.lcxl[row].indexOf(id);
    if (ch >= 0) return `LCXL row ${row + 1} · knob ${ch + 1}`;
  }
  return null;
}

export interface Arc { from: number; to: number; color: string; slot: number }

/** The arcs a knob draws (newest outermost, at most 3) and how many more routes it has. */
export function arcsFor(p: SynthParams, dest: string, base: number): { arcs: Arc[]; more: number } {
  const routes = p.routes.map((r, slot) => ({ r, slot })).filter(({ r }) => r.dest === dest);
  const arcs = routes.slice(-3).reverse().map(({ r, slot }) => ({
    from: base, to: Math.min(1, Math.max(0, base + r.amount)), color: sourceColor(r.source), slot,
  }));
  return { arcs, more: Math.max(0, routes.length - 3) };
}

/** SVG path of an arc on a knob ring (0 = the −135° end, 1 = the +135° end, clockwise). */
export function arcPath(cx: number, cy: number, r: number, n0: number, n1: number): string {
  const a = (n: number) => ((-135 + 270 * n - 90) * Math.PI) / 180;
  const [s, e] = n0 <= n1 ? [n0, n1] : [n1, n0];
  const p = (n: number) => `${(cx + r * Math.cos(a(n))).toFixed(2)} ${(cy + r * Math.sin(a(n))).toFixed(2)}`;
  return `M ${p(s)} A ${r} ${r} 0 ${(e - s) * 270 > 180 ? 1 : 0} 1 ${p(e)}`;
}
```

**`src/lib/synth.test.ts`** (expected values):
- every meta id round-trips: `getParam(init, id)` equals the meta `default` for all 109 params (`init = structuredClone(SYNTH_META.init)`).
- `setParam(p, 'env1.attack', 0.1)` writes `p.env[0].attack`; `setParam(p, 'fx.reverb.mix', 0.4)` writes `p.fx.reverb.mix`; `setParam(p, 'filter.type', 9)` writes `'notch'` and returns 4; `setParam(p, 'oscB.on', 0.7)` writes `true`; `setParam(p, 'oscA.unison', 0)` → 1; `setParam(p, 'filter.cutoff', NaN)` → 20.
- `fromNorm(meta('filter.cutoff'), 0.5)` ≈ 632.456; `toNorm(meta('filter.cutoff'), 12000)` ≈ 0.92605; `fromNorm(meta('env1.attack'), 0.5)` = 1.25; `fromNorm(meta('lfo1.syncDiv'), 1)` = 14.
- `fmtParam`: `('filter.cutoff', 12000)` → `12.0 kHz`; `('filter.cutoff', 632.456)` → `632 Hz`; `('filter.cutoff', 2500)` → `2.50 kHz`; `('lfo1.rate', 0.70711)` → `0.71 Hz`; `('env1.attack', 0.002)` → `2.0 ms`; `('env1.decay', 0.5)` → `500 ms`; `('env1.release', 1.25)` → `1.25 s`; `('oscA.fine', -7)` → `−7 ct`; `('oscA.semi', 0)` → `0 st`; `('voice.volumeDb', -60)` → `−∞`; `('voice.volumeDb', -6)` → `−6.0 dB`; `('oscA.pan', -0.4)` → `L20`; `('filter.envAmt', 0.45)` → `+3.6 oct`; `('filter.drive', 0.5)` → `12.0 dB`; `('lfo1.phase', 0.25)` → `90°`; `('oscA.oct', -1)` → `−1`; `('oscA.unison', 7)` → `7`; `('filter.type', 0)` → `LP 24`; `('oscB.on', 1)` → `ON`; `('oscA.pos', 0.667)` → `67 %`.
- `fmtAmount('osc.pitch', 1 / 60)` → `+0.40 st`; `fmtAmount('filter.cutoff', 0.15)` → `+1.5 oct`; `fmtAmount('oscA.pos', 0.25)` → `+25 %`; `fmtAmount(null, -0.5)` → `−50 %`.
- `arcsFor` with four routes to `filter.cutoff` (amounts 0.1, 0.2, 0.3, 0.9) at base 0.5 → arcs `to` = [1, 0.8, 0.7] (newest first, clamped), `more` = 1, slots [3, 2, 1].
- `arcPath(20, 20, 10, 0, 1)` = `M 12.93 27.07 A 10 10 0 1 1 27.07 27.07`.
- `lcxlTag('filter.cutoff')` = `LCXL row 2 · knob 1`; `lcxlTag('oscA.pan')` = null; `categoryColor('bass')` = [0, 30, 63]; `sourceColor('lfo2')` = `#7f8cff`.

**Knob.svelte** gains optional props (existing callers unchanged): `arcs?: { from: number; to: number; color: string; slot: number }[]` (drawn as `<svg class="mods" aria-hidden="true">` inside `.knob`: one `<path>` per arc on radius `size/2 − 3 − 3·i`, stroke-width 2, the arc's colour, `fill="none"`), `more?: number` (`<span class="mod-more">+{more}</span>` when > 0), `live?: number | null` (a 5 px cream dot `circle` on the outer track at the live position), `selected?: number | null` and `onselect?: (slot: number) => void` (a `.mod-chips` row of 6 px dots under the knob, one per arc, the selected one ringed in `--focus`; clicking a dot selects), `tag?: string` / `tagTitle?: string` (`<span class="xl-tag" title={tagTitle}>{tag}</span>` at the knob's top right, `font: 600 8px var(--f-silk); color: var(--vec)`), `dest?: string` (rendered as `data-dest` on `.knob`, the drop target of Task 16), `onalt?: (delta: number) => void` (pointer drags with Alt held call `onalt(Δ)` in knob units, Shift fine, instead of changing the value), `menuExtra?: () => MenuItem[]` (passed to `learnable`).

**learnable.ts:** `LearnableOptions` gains `extra?: () => MenuItem[]`; the menu becomes `[...extra(), (a separator when extra is non-empty), Learn MIDI CC, Reset]`.

**ParamKnob.svelte** (`{ id, params, live = null, size, label?, onparam, onalt?, selected = null, onselect?, menuExtra? }`): wraps `Knob` in knob space — `value = toNorm(meta, getParam(params, id))`, `min 0`, `max 1`, `def = toNorm(meta, meta.default)`, `step = 1 / (max − min)` for `int` / `choice` kinds else `0.01`, `fine 0.001`, `fmt = (n) => fmtParam(id, fromNorm(meta, n))`, `ariaLabel = "{section} {label}"`, `learnId = "synth.{id}"`, `arcs`/`more` from `arcsFor(params, dest, value)` when the control is a destination, `tag = 'XL'` and `tagTitle = lcxlTag(id)` when mapped, `dest = destOf(id)`, `onchange = (n) => onparam(id, fromNorm(meta, n))`.

**ParamFader.svelte** (`{ id, params, height, label?, onparam }`): a vertical fader in knob space (linear travel, no dB law), `role="slider"`, `aria-valuetext = fmtParam(…)`, arrows / Shift fine / double-click = default / wheel, `learnId = "synth.{id}"`, a value LCD under it; markup and CSS modelled on `Fader.svelte` (`.fader`, `.f-slot`, `.f-cap`; width 20 px, cap 22 px tall).

**ParamStep.svelte** (`{ id, params, onparam }`): `NumLcd` with `min`/`max` from the meta, `step 1`, `def` = meta default, `fmt = (v) => fmtParam(id, v)`, label `"{section} {label}"`, no learn id (integers are not learnable).

**ChoiceLcd.svelte** (`{ id, params, onparam, width? }`): a `.lcd.screen` button showing `fmtParam(id, value)`; click, Enter or Space opens `ContextMenu` with every label (current ticked) → `onparam(id, index)`; `aria-haspopup="menu"`, title `"{section} {label} — click to change"`.

**ParamKey.svelte** (`{ id, params, label?, onparam }`): a `.key.small` toggle. For `bool`: `class:on`, `aria-pressed`, text = `label ?? meta.label`, click → `onparam(id, value ? 0 : 1)`. For a two-choice selector (POL): text = the current label (BI / UNI), click flips. `learnId = "synth.{id}"` for bools only.

### Task 15: Synth sections and pages

**Purpose.** Spec 6.3 (sections, pages, controls), 9 (dim overlay), the optimistic edit path of spec 2.2.

- Create: `src/components/SynthOsc.svelte`, `SynthSubNoise.svelte`, `SynthFilter.svelte`, `SynthAmpVoice.svelte`, `SynthEnv.svelte`, `SynthLfo.svelte`, `SynthFx.svelte`
- Modify: `src/components/SynthPlate.svelte`, `src/App.svelte`, `src/styles/app.css` (shared section classes)

**Shared props** of every section: `{ params: SynthParams; live: number[] | null; knob: number; onparam: (id: string, value: number) => void }` (Task 16 adds the mod callbacks). Every section is `<section class="syn-sec" aria-label="…">` with `<header class="syn-title"><span class="silk">TITLE</span>…</header>`, a display `<div class="syn-disp screen" aria-hidden="true"><canvas></canvas></div>` where the spec has one (Task 17 draws it), and `<div class="syn-ctl">` with the controls in this order:

| Section (title) | Controls (ids; `K` knob, `L` ChoiceLcd, `S` ParamStep, `Y` ParamKey, `F` ParamFader) |
|---|---|
| `SynthOsc` x = A, B ("OSC A") | title: Y `oscX.on`; display; L `oscX.table`; K `oscX.pos` (size 1.2 × knob); L `oscX.warp`; K `oscX.warpAmt`; S `oscX.unison`; K `oscX.detune`, `oscX.blend`, `oscX.width`, `oscX.phaseRand`; S `oscX.oct`, `oscX.semi`; K `oscX.fine`, `oscX.level`, `oscX.pan`; Y `oscX.toFilter` |
| `SynthSubNoise` ("SUB · NOISE") | Y `sub.on` (label SUB); L `sub.shape`; S `sub.oct`; K `sub.level`; Y `sub.toFilter`; Y `noise.on` (label NOISE); L `noise.color`; K `noise.level`; Y `noise.toFilter` |
| `SynthFilter` ("FILTER") | title: Y `filter.on`; display; L `filter.type`; K `filter.cutoff` (1.2 × knob), `filter.reso`, `filter.drive`, `filter.keyTrack`, `filter.envAmt` |
| `SynthAmpVoice` ("AMP" and "VOICE", two titled halves) | K `amp.level`, `amp.velSens`; L `voice.mode`; K `voice.glide`; L `voice.glideMode`; S `voice.bendRange`; K `voice.drift`, `voice.volumeDb` |
| `SynthEnv` n = 1, 2, 3 ("ENV1 · AMP", "ENV2 · FILTER", "ENV3") | display; F `envN.attack`, `envN.decay`, `envN.sustain`, `envN.release` (labels A D S R) |
| `SynthLfo` n = 1, 2 ("LFO1", "LFO2") | display; L `lfoN.shape`; K `lfoN.syncDiv` labelled RATE when `lfoN.sync` is on, else K `lfoN.rate`; Y `lfoN.sync`; L `lfoN.mode`; K `lfoN.delay`, `lfoN.phase`; Y `lfoN.polarity` |
| `SynthFx` (one unit per prop `unit`: DRIVE, CHORUS, DELAY, REVERB) | DRIVE: Y `fx.drive.on`, L `fx.drive.type`, K amount, tone, mix · CHORUS: Y on, K rate, depth, delay, feedback, mix · DELAY: Y on, K `fx.delay.syncDiv` labelled TIME when sync is on else K `fx.delay.time`, Y sync, K feedback, Y pingPong, K tone, mix · REVERB: Y on, K size, decay, damp, preDelay, mix |

```ts
// Synth edits are optimistic: the set shows the value now, the backend stores it (spec 2.2).
function synthParam(id: string, value: number) {
  const stored = setParam(currentSet.keys.patch.params, id, value);
  currentSet.keys.modified = true;
  void invoke('synth_param', { id, value: stored }).catch((e) => { showToast(errorText(e)); void resyncSet(); });
}
```
