**Additions to `src/lib/synth.ts`:**

```ts
/** Leading + trailing throttle: at most one call per `ms`, the last arguments always delivered. */
export function throttle<A extends unknown[]>(fn: (...args: A) => void, ms = 16): (...args: A) => void {
  let last = 0;
  let timer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined;
  let pending: A | null = null;
  return (...args: A) => {
    const now = Date.now();
    if (now - last >= ms) { last = now; pending = null; fn(...args); return; }
    pending = args;
    timer ??= setTimeout(() => {
      timer = undefined;
      last = Date.now();
      if (pending) { const p = pending; pending = null; fn(...p); }
    }, ms - (now - last));
  };
}

/** Selected-route indices after removing `slot` (later slots move up by one). */
export function withoutRoute(selected: Record<string, number>, slot: number): Record<string, number> {
  const next: Record<string, number> = {};
  for (const [dest, s] of Object.entries(selected)) {
    if (s < slot) next[dest] = s;
    else if (s > slot) next[dest] = s - 1;
  }
  return next;
}
```

Tests: `withoutRoute({ a: 0, b: 2, c: 3 }, 2)` → `{ a: 0, c: 2 }`; `throttle` (with `node:test`'s `mock.timers`): three calls within 16 ms → `fn` called twice, the second time with the third call's arguments.

**ModSourceStrip.svelte** (`{ meters: number[] | null; onstart: (source: SourceId, e: PointerEvent) => void }`): `<div class="mod-strip" role="toolbar" aria-label="Modulation sources">` with one `<button class="mod-badge" style:--c={color}>` per `SYNTH_META.sources` entry: `<span class="grip" aria-hidden="true">≡</span>{label}<i class="mod-meter" style:--m={meter}></i>`; `title="{label} — drag onto a knob to modulate it"`; `pointerdown` (left button) → `onstart(id, e)`. The meter shows `sources[i]` mapped to 0–1 (`(v + 1) / 2` for LFOs, NOTE, RAND and BEND; the value itself for the others; 0 without a feed). CSS: `.mod-strip { display: flex; gap: 4px; flex-wrap: wrap; }` `.mod-badge { display: inline-flex; align-items: center; gap: 4px; height: 20px; padding: 0 6px; border: 0; border-radius: 3px; background: var(--glass); box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 1px var(--c); color: var(--c); font: 600 9.5px/1 var(--f-silk); letter-spacing: .1em; cursor: grab; position: relative; }` `.mod-meter { position: absolute; left: 3px; right: 3px; bottom: 2px; height: 2px; background: var(--c); transform-origin: left; transform: scaleX(var(--m, 0)); opacity: .8; }`.

**Drag and drop** (in `SynthPlate`; the ENV and LFO section titles call the same `onstart` with `env1`…`lfo2`): pointer capture on the source; after a 4 px move a `DragGhost` follows the pointer (`label` = source label, `color` = source colour); the `[data-dest]` element under the pointer (`document.elementFromPoint` → `closest('[data-dest]')`) gets the class `drop-ok` (removed when the pointer leaves it); pointer up over one → `onaddroute(source, dest)`; anywhere else, Esc (`pushEsc`) or `pointercancel` → nothing. `app.css`: `.drop-ok { outline: 2px solid var(--warn); outline-offset: 3px; }`.

**App.svelte:**

```ts
let selectedRoute = $state<Record<string, number>>({});
const routes = () => currentSet.keys.patch.params.routes;
function sendRoute(slot: number, route: Route | null) {
  currentSet.keys.modified = true;
  void invoke('synth_route', { slot, route }).catch((e) => { showToast(errorText(e)); void resyncSet(); });
}
const sendRouteSoon = throttle(sendRoute, 16);
function addRoute(source: SourceId, dest: string) {
  if (routes().length >= 16) { showToast('Matrix full (16 routes)'); return; }
  const route: Route = { source, dest, amount: 0.25, aux: null, bypass: false };
  routes().push(route);
  selectedRoute[dest] = routes().length - 1;
  sendRoute(routes().length - 1, route);
}
function updateRoute(slot: number, route: Route) {
  routes()[slot] = route;
  sendRouteSoon(slot, route);
}
function removeRoute(slot: number) {
  routes().splice(slot, 1);
  selectedRoute = withoutRoute(selectedRoute, slot);
  sendRoute(slot, null);
}
/** Alt+drag on a knob: the depth of its selected route (else its newest). */
function routeDepth(dest: string, delta: number) {
  const slots = routes().map((r, i) => (r.dest === dest ? i : -1)).filter((i) => i >= 0);
  const slot = slots.includes(selectedRoute[dest]) ? selectedRoute[dest] : slots.at(-1);
  if (slot === undefined) return;
  const r = routes()[slot];
  updateRoute(slot, { ...r, amount: Math.min(1, Math.max(-1, r.amount + delta)) });
}
```

`sendRouteSoon` is throttled per call site; a route add or delete is never throttled. `ParamKnob` gets `onalt={(d) => routeDepth(dest, d)}`, `selected={selectedRoute[dest]}`, `onselect={(slot) => (selectedRoute[dest] = slot)}` and `menuExtra` = one item per route on that knob: `Remove {SOURCE} ({fmtAmount(dest, amount)})` → `removeRoute(slot)`.

**SynthMatrix.svelte** (`{ params: SynthParams; meters: number[] | null; onupdate: (slot, route) => void; onremove: (slot) => void; onadd: () => void }`; App's `onadd` appends `{ source: 'env2', dest: null, amount: 0.25, aux: null, bypass: false }` like `addRoute` without a destination): a scrolling `<div class="matrix" role="table">` (K8) with a header row (SOURCE · DESTINATION · AMOUNT · AUX · BYP · · ×, `.silk`) and one row per route: a source LCD (menu of the 10 sources), a destination LCD (menu: one info row per section — OSC A, OSC B, OSC, SUB, NOISE, FILTER, AMP, LFO1, LFO2 — then its destinations by `destLabel`; text `—` while `dest` is null), an amount `NumLcd` (`min −1`, `max 1`, `step 0.01`, `def 0.25`, `fmt = (v) => fmtAmount(dest, v)`), an aux LCD (menu `—` + the sources), a `BYP` `.key.small` (`aria-pressed`), the source's live meter, a `×` `.key.small` (`aria-label="Delete route {n}"`). Below: `<button class="key small">+ ADD ROUTE</button>` (`aria-disabled` at 16 routes). Every edit calls `onupdate(slot, nextRoute)`.

**synthFeed.ts** (complete):

```ts
// The synth's live feed (`synth-telemetry`, keys synth spec 2.6): latest frame, smoothed spectrum, listeners.
import { specLevel } from './telemetry.ts';
import type { SynthTelemetry } from './synth-types.ts';

const BANDS = 128;
const FALL = 0.88;
type Listener = () => void;
const listeners = new Set<Listener>();

export const synthFeed = { frame: null as SynthTelemetry | null, at: -Infinity, spectrum: new Float32Array(BANDS) };

/** True while frames arrive (the last one under 500 ms old). */
export const synthLive = (now = performance.now()) => synthFeed.frame !== null && now - synthFeed.at < 500;

export function onSynthFeed(listener: Listener): () => void {
  listeners.add(listener);
  return () => { listeners.delete(listener); };
}

/** A `synth-telemetry` event arrived: bars rise at once and fall 12 % per frame. */
export function pushSynthFrame(frame: SynthTelemetry, now = performance.now()): void {
  synthFeed.frame = frame;
  synthFeed.at = now;
  for (let i = 0; i < BANDS; i++) synthFeed.spectrum[i] = Math.max(specLevel(frame.spectrum[i] ?? -100), synthFeed.spectrum[i] * FALL);
  listeners.forEach((l) => l());
}
```

**synthDraw.ts** — pure helpers (complete) plus the draw functions:

```ts
// Drawing for the synth displays (keys synth spec 7). Helpers are pure; draw* paint into a phosphor canvas.
import type { EnvParams, LfoParams, LfoShape } from './synth-types.ts';

export const freqToX = (hz: number, w: number) => (Math.log(hz / 20) / Math.log(1000)) * w;
export const xToFreq = (x: number, w: number) => 20 * Math.pow(1000, x / w);
/** dB to y on the FILTER grid (+24 dB at the top, −36 dB at the bottom). */
export const dbToY = (db: number, h: number, top = 24, bottom = -36) => ((top - db) / (top - bottom)) * h;

/** The display's time model: attack, decay, a sustain hold (a quarter of A + D + R, at least 50 ms), release. */
function envModel(e: EnvParams) {
  const a = Math.max(e.attack, 0.0005), d = Math.max(e.decay, 0), r = Math.max(e.release, 0.0005);
  const hold = Math.max(0.05, 0.25 * (a + d + r));
  return { a, d, r, hold, total: a + d + hold + r, s: Math.min(1, Math.max(0, e.sustain)), tau: a / Math.log(1.3 / 0.3) };
}

/** n points [x 0–1, level 0–1] of the ADSR curve, drawn with the engine's curves (spec 3.7). */
export function envPoints(e: EnvParams, n: number): [number, number][] {
  const m = envModel(e);
  const level = (t: number): number => {
    if (t <= m.a) return Math.min(1, 1.3 * (1 - Math.exp(-t / m.tau)));
    t -= m.a;
    if (t <= m.d) return m.d > 0 ? m.s + (1 - m.s) * Math.exp((Math.log(0.01) * t) / m.d) : m.s;
    t -= m.d;
    if (t <= m.hold) return m.s;
    t -= m.hold;
    return m.s * Math.exp((Math.log(0.01) * t) / m.r);
  };
  return Array.from({ length: n }, (_, i) => { const x = i / (n - 1); return [x, level(x * m.total)] as [number, number]; });
}

/** Where the live dot sits (x 0–1) for a monitor stage (1 attack … 4 release) and level; null when idle. */
export function envDotX(stage: number, level: number, e: EnvParams): number | null {
  const m = envModel(e);
  const at = (t: number) => Math.min(1, Math.max(0, t / m.total));
  switch (Math.round(stage)) {
    case 1: return at(-m.tau * Math.log(Math.max(1e-6, 1 - Math.min(level, 1.29) / 1.3)));
    case 2: return m.d > 0 && level > m.s ? at(m.a + (m.d * Math.log((level - m.s) / (1 - m.s))) / Math.log(0.01)) : at(m.a + m.d);
    case 3: return at(m.a + m.d + m.hold / 2);
    case 4: return m.s > 0 && level > 0 ? at(m.a + m.d + m.hold + (m.r * Math.log(level / m.s)) / Math.log(0.01)) : 1;
    default: return null;
  }
}

const STEPS = [0.6, -0.2, 0.9, -0.7, 0.1, -0.9, 0.4, -0.4];

/** −1…1 at `phase`; the random shapes draw a fixed example row of eight steps. */
export function lfoValue(shape: LfoShape, phase: number): number {
  const p = ((phase % 1) + 1) % 1;
  switch (shape) {
    case 'sine': return Math.sin(2 * Math.PI * p);
    case 'triangle': return p < 0.25 ? 4 * p : p < 0.75 ? 2 - 4 * p : 4 * p - 4;
    case 'sawUp': return 2 * p - 1;
    case 'sawDown': return 1 - 2 * p;
    case 'square': return p < 0.5 ? 1 : -1;
    case 'sampleHold': return STEPS[Math.floor(p * 8) % 8];
    default: {
      const i = Math.floor(p * 8) % 8, f = p * 8 - Math.floor(p * 8);
      return STEPS[i] + (STEPS[(i + 1) % 8] - STEPS[i]) * (0.5 - 0.5 * Math.cos(Math.PI * f));
    }
  }
}

/** One cycle from the start PHASE, with polarity applied. */
export function lfoPoints(l: LfoParams, n: number): number[] {
  return Array.from({ length: n }, (_, i) => {
    const v = lfoValue(l.shape, l.phase + i / n);
    return l.polarity === 'uni' ? 0.5 * (v + 1) : v;
  });
}
```

Draw functions (same file; each starts from the `fit()` context, draws, and the caller `present()`s):
- `drawWavetable(g, W, H, frames, pos, color)`: up to 24 frames (evenly picked) as polylines from back (top-right, alpha .15) to front (bottom-left, alpha .5), each offset by `(0.25 W, −0.35 H) × depth`; the frame nearest `pos` redrawn in cream `#ece3cc`; `null` frames → `txt(g, 'STANDBY', …)`.
- `drawFilter(g, W, H, curve, spectrum, cutoffHz, color)`: grid lines at +12, 0, −12, −24 dB (`rgba(65,200,255,.22)`) and labels `100`, `1k`, `10k`; the spectrum as a filled area (`rgba(65,200,255,.25)`, heights from `synthFeed.spectrum`); the curve as a 2 px polyline in `color` (`#ffb21a`); a dashed vertical cutoff marker; `null` curve → STANDBY.
- `drawEnv(g, W, H, e, dot, color)`: `envPoints(e, 96)` as a polyline in `color` with a faint fill; the dot (`envDotX`, level) in cream.
- `drawLfo(g, W, H, l, phase, color)`: `lfoPoints(l, 96)` (bipolar centred, unipolar from the bottom) and a cream dot at `phase`.
- `drawScope(g, W, H, samples)`: a 1.5 px green trace (`#7dffb0`) of the 256 samples, no afterglow; `null` → a flat line and STANDBY.

 *  10 Hz with reduced motion) and when `key` changes; nothing while the window is hidden. */
export function display(node: HTMLCanvasElement, opts: { draw: (g: CanvasRenderingContext2D, w: number, h: number) => void; key?: unknown }): { update(next: typeof opts): void; destroy(): void };
/** The display frames of a table, fetched once (`synth_table`); null in the preview. */

```ts
// The patch menu (keys synth spec 8.4) as plain menu items; App supplies the actions. Pure.
import type { MenuItem } from '../components/ContextMenu.svelte';
import { SYNTH_META } from './synth-meta.ts';
import type { KeysSettings, PatchRef, PatchSource } from './synth-types.ts';

export type PatchRequest = { factory: string } | { user: string } | { init: true };
export interface PatchActions {
  load(request: PatchRequest): void; save(): void; saveAs(): void; rename(): void; remove(): void;
  revert(): void; showFolder(): void; openFactory(): void; openUser(): void;
}

const isUser = (s: PatchSource): s is { user: string } => typeof s === 'object' && 'user' in s;
const isFactory = (s: PatchSource): s is { factory: string } => typeof s === 'object' && 'factory' in s;

/** The patch LCD text: the name, plus ` *` while modified. */
export const patchLabel = (k: KeysSettings) => (k.modified ? `${k.patch.name} *` : k.patch.name);

export function patchMenuItems(k: KeysSettings, userCount: number, a: PatchActions): MenuItem[] {
  return [
    { label: 'FACTORY ▸', action: a.openFactory },
    { label: 'USER ▸', action: a.openUser, disabled: userCount === 0 },
    { label: '', sep: true },
    { label: 'SAVE', action: a.save },
    { label: 'SAVE AS…', action: a.saveAs },
    { label: 'RENAME…', action: a.rename, disabled: !isUser(k.patchSource) },
    { label: 'DELETE', action: a.remove, disabled: !isUser(k.patchSource) },
    { label: '', sep: true },
    { label: 'INIT', action: () => a.load({ init: true }) },
    { label: 'REVERT', action: a.revert, disabled: !k.modified },
    { label: '', sep: true },
    { label: 'SHOW FOLDER', action: a.showFolder },
  ];
}

/** Factory patches grouped under their category (spec 8.4 FACTORY ▸). */
export function factoryItems(list: PatchRef[], current: PatchSource, load: (r: PatchRequest) => void): MenuItem[] {
  const items: MenuItem[] = [];
  for (const cat of SYNTH_META.categories) {
    const inCat = list.filter((p) => p.category === cat.id);
    if (!inCat.length) continue;
    items.push({ label: cat.label, info: true });
    for (const p of inCat) items.push({ label: p.name, checked: isFactory(current) && current.factory === p.id, action: () => load({ factory: p.id }) });
  }
  return items;
}

const byName = (a: PatchRef, b: PatchRef) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name);

/** User patches, alphabetical (spec 8.4 USER ▸). */
export function userItems(list: PatchRef[], current: PatchSource, load: (r: PatchRequest) => void): MenuItem[] {
  return [...list].sort(byName).map((p) => ({ label: p.name, checked: isUser(current) && current.user === p.id, action: () => load({ user: p.id }) }));
}

/** ◀ ▶: the factory list then the user list, wrapping; from INIT (or a source not in the lists) the first or last. */
export function neighbour(lists: { factory: PatchRef[]; user: PatchRef[] }, current: PatchSource, step: 1 | -1): PatchRequest | null {
  const all: PatchRequest[] = [...lists.factory.map((p) => ({ factory: p.id })), ...[...lists.user].sort(byName).map((p) => ({ user: p.id }))];
  if (!all.length) return null;
  const i = all.findIndex((r) => ('factory' in r && isFactory(current) && current.factory === r.factory)
    || ('user' in r && isUser(current) && current.user === r.user));
  if (i < 0) return all[step > 0 ? 0 : all.length - 1];
  return all[(i + step + all.length) % all.length];
}
```

**`src/lib/patchMenu.test.ts`:** `patchLabel` with and without `modified`; for a factory source RENAME and DELETE are disabled and REVERT is disabled until `modified`; for a user source they are enabled; USER ▸ is disabled with 0 user patches; `factoryItems(all 16 refs, { factory: 'organ' })` starts with the info row `PIANO`, has 8 info rows (one per category in `SYNTH_META.categories` order that has patches — all 8 do) and ticks only ORGAN; `userItems` sorts names; `neighbour`: from the last factory patch `+1` → the first user patch, from the first factory patch `−1` → the last user patch, from `'init'` `+1` → the first factory patch, empty lists → null.

**ContextMenu.svelte:** add `max-height: calc(100vh - 8px); overflow-y: auto;` to `.menu` (long patch lists scroll; small menus are unaffected).

**PatchNamePrompt.svelte** (`{ title: 'SAVE AS' | 'RENAME'; initial: string; error: string | null; x: number; y: number; onsubmit: (name: string) => void; oncancel: () => void }`): a fixed `.screen` popover: `.silk` title, an input (`maxlength="24"`, shown uppercase, Enter = submit, Esc = cancel, autofocus with the text selected), SAVE and CANCEL `.key.small`; `error` shows under the input in `--error` (the "exists" case shows "Exists — SAVE again to replace").

**App.svelte patch actions** (shared by the synth header and the keys plate; all through `invoke`, all refreshing the set they return):
- `load(r)` → `synth_load_patch({ source: r })`; errors toast their text ("Patch not loaded — …").
- `save()` → for a user source `synth_save_patch({ name: patch.name, overwrite: true })`, otherwise opens SAVE AS.
- SAVE AS submit → `synth_save_patch({ name, overwrite: retry })`; error `exists` → prompt error "Exists — SAVE again to replace" and the next submit sends `overwrite: true`; success → `confirm('Saved {NAME}')` and refetch the set (`get_current_set`).
- RENAME submit → `synth_rename_patch({ file, name })`; DELETE → `synth_delete_patch({ file })` then refetch the set; REVERT → `synth_revert()` (error "Patch file not found" toasts); SHOW FOLDER → `patches_folder()` then `reveal_path({ path })`.
- FACTORY ▸ / USER ▸ → `synth_patches()` then a second `ContextMenu` at the same position with `factoryItems` / `userItems`. ◀ ▶ → `neighbour(...)` then `load`.
- `keys-notice` → `showToast(event.text)` (K18).

**Synth header (SynthPlate line 1):** the patch LCD (`patchLabel`, text colour = `ledToCss(categoryColor(patch.category))`, click / Enter = the patch menu), `◀` and `▶` `.key.small` (`aria-label` "Previous patch" / "Next patch"), `SAVE` `.key.small`, `▾` `.key.small` (the menu), then the page keys, then `VOICES` / `CPU` LCDs and the engine lamp (`.lamp.ok` for SYNTH; off for SOUNDFONT).

**KeysPlate.svelte (K14 ends):** with `engine === 'synth'` the sound LCD shows `patchLabel(keys)` in the category colour and opens the patch menu (`onpatchmenu(e)` prop); held keys light in the category colour. With `engine === 'soundfont'` it keeps the Task 9 behaviour (GM list, `gmSound`, `SOUND_COLORS`). The title reads "Keys patch: {name} — click to change. Notes learned to a pad play that pad." for the synth.

**SetupKeys.svelte:** row `ENGINE`: a `.switch` with SYNTH and SOUNDFONT keys (`.on` = current; SOUNDFONT `aria-disabled` and titled "Load a SoundFont first" without a `.sf2`) → `onengine(engine)` (App: `set_keys_engine`); row `SOUNDFONT` as today (LOAD… → `set_soundfont`, which now also switches to SOUNDFONT; CLEAR switches back to SYNTH, K6); row `PATCHES`: the folder path LCD (`patches_folder`) and a `SHOW` key; hint `The synth plays the keys; a General MIDI SoundFont (.sf2) can replace it.`
